--- ФАЙЛ: ./next.config.js ---

/** @type {import('next').NextConfig} */
const nextConfig = {
  reactStrictMode: true,
  async rewrites() {
    return [
      {
        source: '/api/:path*',
        destination: 'http://backend:3000/api/:path*',
      },
      {
        source: '/auth/:path*',
        destination: 'http://backend:3000/api/auth/:path*',
      },
      {
        source: '/media/:path*',
        destination: 'http://backend:3000/media/:path*',
      }
    ];
  },
};

module.exports = nextConfig;


--- ФАЙЛ: ./export_code.py ---

import os

# Настройки экспорта
ROOT_DIR = '.'
OUTPUT_FILE = 'project_context.txt'

# Исключения, чтобы не засорять вывод
IGNORE_DIRS = {'.git', 'node_modules', '.next', 'dist', 'build', '.vscode', 'venv', '__pycache__', 'out'}
IGNORE_FILES = {'package-lock.json', 'yarn.lock', 'pnpm-lock.yaml', 'project_context.txt'}
IGNORE_EXTS = {
    '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.svg', '.ico', '.webp', # Изображения
    '.mp3', '.wav', '.mp4',                           # Медиа
    '.woff', '.woff2', '.ttf', '.eot',                # Шрифты
    '.pdf', '.zip', '.tar', '.gz', '.exe'             # Архивы и бинарники
}

def is_valid_file(file_name):
    if file_name in IGNORE_FILES: return False
    if any(file_name.endswith(ext) for ext in IGNORE_EXTS): return False
    return True

with open(OUTPUT_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as out:
    out.write("=== СТРУКТУРА ПРОЕКТА ===\n")
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(ROOT_DIR):
        # Исключаем ненужные директории
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
        
        level = root.replace(ROOT_DIR, '').count(os.sep)
        indent = ' ' * 4 * level
        out.write(f"{indent}{os.path.basename(root)}/\n")
        
        subindent = ' ' * 4 * (level + 1)
        for f in files:
            out.write(f"{subindent}{f}\n")

    out.write("\n\n=== ИСХОДНЫЙ КОД ===\n")
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(ROOT_DIR):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
        
        for file in files:
            if not is_valid_file(file):
                continue
                
            file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
            try:
                with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    content = f.read()
                out.write(f"\n\n--- ФАЙЛ: {file_path} ---\n\n")
                out.write(content)
            except Exception as e:
                out.write(f"\n\n--- ФАЙЛ: {file_path} (Пропущен: невозможно прочитать как текст) ---\n")

print(f"Готово! Данные сохранены в {OUTPUT_FILE}")


--- ФАЙЛ: ./Dockerfile ---

FROM node:20-bookworm-slim

RUN sed -i 's/deb.debian.org/mirror.yandex.ru/g' /etc/apt/sources.list.d/debian.sources 2>/dev/null || \
    sed -i 's/deb.debian.org/mirror.yandex.ru/g' /etc/apt/sources.list 2>/dev/null

WORKDIR /app

COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci

COPY . .

ARG NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL
ENV NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=$NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL

RUN npm run build

EXPOSE 3000

CMD ["npm", "start"]

'use client';

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Ban, Check, RefreshCw, ScanLine, Trash2, Upload } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { Paged, Track } from '@/lib/types';
import { cn, formatBytes, formatDate, formatDuration } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';

type Tab = 'stats' | 'review' | 'users';

interface Stats {
  tracks: number;
  needsReview: number;
  users: number;
  genres: number;
  withoutCover: number;
  withoutGenre: number;
  libraryBytes: number;
  totalDurationSec: number;
  disk: { freeBytes: number; totalBytes: number } | null;
  topArtists: { artist: string; tracks: number }[];
}

interface ScanState {
  running: boolean;
  total: number;
  processed: number;
  created: number;
  updated: number;
  skipped: number;
  duplicates: number;
  needsReview: number;
  errors: number;
  missing: number;
  pruned: number;
  errorSamples: string[];
  finishedAt: string | null;
}

interface AdminUser {
  id: string;
  username: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'ADMIN' | 'USER';
  canUpload: boolean;
  isBlocked: boolean;
  createdAt: string;
  playlists: number;
  favorites: number;
}

interface Summary {
  needsReview: number;
}

const Err = ({ msg }: { msg: string | null }) => (msg ? <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{msg}</div> : null);

/* ---------------- Статистика и сканирование ---------------- */

function StatsTab() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats | null>(null);
  const [scan, setScan] = useState<ScanState | null>(null);
  const [prune, setPrune] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadStats = useCallback(() => {
    api<Stats>('/admin/stats').then(setStats).catch((e: Error) => setError(e.message));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStats();
    api<ScanState>('/admin/scan').then(setScan).catch(() => undefined);
  }, [loadStats]);

  // пока идёт сканирование — опрашиваем прогресс
  const running = scan?.running;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return;
    const t = setInterval(() => {
      api<ScanState>('/admin/scan')
        .then((s) => {
          setScan(s);
          if (!s.running) loadStats();
        })
        .catch(() => undefined);
    }, 1500);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, [running, loadStats]);

  const startScan = async () => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      setScan(await api<ScanState>('/admin/scan', { method: 'POST', body: { pruneMissing: prune } }));
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };

  const enrich = async () => {
    try {
      await api('/admin/enrich', { method: 'POST' });
      setMsg('Обогащение метаданных поставлено в очередь');
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };

  const runCleanup = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api<Record<string, unknown>>('/admin/cleanup', { method: 'POST' });
      setMsg(`Чистка завершена: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
      loadStats();
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };

  const cards = stats
    ? [
        ['Треков', stats.tracks],
        ['Нужна проверка', stats.needsReview],
        ['Пользователей', stats.users],
        ['Жанров', stats.genres],
        ['Без обложки', stats.withoutCover],
        ['Без жанра', stats.withoutGenre],
        ['Длительность', `${Math.round(stats.totalDurationSec / 3600)} ч`],
      ]
    : [];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <Err msg={error} />
      {stats && (
        <>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">
            {cards.map(([label, value]) => (
              <div key={label} className="card p-4">
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{value}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{label}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="card space-y-3 p-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
              <span>Размер библиотеки</span>
              <span className="font-medium">{formatBytes(stats.libraryBytes)}</span>
            </div>
            {stats.disk && (
              <>
                <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded bg-zinc-800">
                  <div
                    className="h-full bg-accent"
                    style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, ((stats.disk.totalBytes - stats.disk.freeBytes) / stats.disk.totalBytes) * 100)}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                  Диск: свободно {formatBytes(stats.disk.freeBytes)} из {formatBytes(stats.disk.totalBytes)}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
            {stats.topArtists.length > 0 && (
              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                Больше всего треков: {stats.topArtists.map((a) => `${a.artist} (${a.tracks})`).join(', ')}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <div className="card space-y-3 p-4">
        <h3 className="font-semibold">Сканирование и обслуживание</h3>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={startScan} disabled={scan?.running}>
            <ScanLine size={16} /> {scan?.running ? 'Сканирую…' : 'Запустить сканирование'}
          </button>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={prune} onChange={(e) => setPrune(e.target.checked)} /> Удалять из базы пропавшие файлы
          </label>
          <button className="btn" onClick={enrich}>
            <RefreshCw size={16} /> Обогатить метаданные
          </button>
          <button className="btn" onClick={runCleanup}>
            <Trash2 size={16} /> Очистить мусор БД/диска
          </button>
        </div>
        {msg && <div className="text-sm text-yellow-300">{msg}</div>}
        {scan && (scan.running || scan.finishedAt) && (
          <div className="space-y-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">
            {scan.running && (
              <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded bg-zinc-800">
                <div className="h-full bg-accent transition-all" style={{ width: `${scan.total ? (scan.processed / scan.total) * 100 : 5}%` }} />
              </div>
            )}
            <div>
              Обработано {scan.processed} из {scan.total} · новых {scan.created} · обновлено {scan.updated} · без изменений {scan.skipped} · дубликатов{' '}
              {scan.duplicates} · на проверку {scan.needsReview} · ошибок {scan.errors}
              {scan.missing > 0 && ` · пропало с диска ${scan.missing}${scan.pruned ? ` (удалено из базы: ${scan.pruned})` : ''}`}
            </div>
            {scan.errorSamples.length > 0 && <pre className="overflow-x-auto rounded bg-zinc-900 p-2 text-xs text-red-300">{scan.errorSamples.join('\n')}</pre>}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------- Нераспознанные треки ---------------- */

function ReviewRow({ track, onSaved }: { track: Track; onSaved: (id: string) => void }) {
  const [artist, setArtist] = useState(track.artist === 'Unknown Artist' ? '' : track.artist);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(track.title);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const save = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await api(`/admin/tracks/${track.id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body: { artist: artist.trim(), title: title.trim() } });
      onSaved(track.id);
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const remove = async () => {
    if (!confirm('Удалить трек из базы? Файл на диске останется.')) return;
    await api(`/admin/tracks/${track.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    onSaved(track.id);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2 px-4 py-3">
      <div className="truncate text-xs text-zinc-500" title={track.filePath}>
        {track.owner ? `${track.owner} · ` : ''}{track.filePath?.split(/[\\/]/).pop()} · {formatDuration(track.duration)} · {formatBytes(track.fileSize ?? 0)}
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <input className="input !w-auto min-w-0 flex-1" placeholder="Исполнитель" value={artist} onChange={(e) => setArtist(e.target.value)} />
        <input className="input !w-auto min-w-0 flex-[2]" placeholder="Название" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} />
        <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={save} disabled={busy || !artist.trim() || !title.trim()}>
          <Check size={16} /> Сохранить
        </button>
        <button className="btn" onClick={remove} title="Удалить из базы">
          <Trash2 size={16} />
        </button>
      </div>
      <Err msg={error} />
    </div>
  );
}

function ReviewTab() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<Paged<Track> | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const load = useCallback(() => {
    api<Paged<Track>>('/admin/review', { query: { limit: 100 } }).then(setData).catch((e: Error) => setError(e.message));
  }, []);
  useEffect(load, [load]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Файлы, имя которых не соответствует «Исполнитель - Название» и в тегах которых нет исполнителя и названия.</p>
      <Err msg={error} />
      {data && data.items.length === 0 && <div className="card px-6 py-12 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Нераспознанных треков нет 🎉</div>}
      {data && data.items.length > 0 && (
        <div className="card divide-y divide-zinc-900">
          {data.items.map((t) => (
            <ReviewRow key={t.id} track={t} onSaved={(id) => setData((d) => d && { ...d, total: d.total - 1, items: d.items.filter((x) => x.id !== id) })} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------- Пользователи ---------------- */

function UsersTab() {
  const me = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState<AdminUser[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    api<{ items: AdminUser[] }>('/admin/users').then((r) => setUsers(r.items)).catch((e: Error) => setError(e.message));
  }, []);
  useEffect(load, [load]);

  const patch = async (id: string, body: Partial<Pick<AdminUser, 'isBlocked' | 'canUpload' | 'role'>>) => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await api(`/admin/users/${id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body });
      load();
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };

  const remove = async (u: AdminUser) => {
    if (!confirm(`Удалить пользователя ${u.username} вместе с его плейлистами и избранным?`)) return;
    try {
      await api(`/admin/users/${u.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      load();
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Err msg={error} />
      {users && (
        <div className="card overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="text-left text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-2">Пользователь</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2">Роль</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2">Загрузка</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2">Регистрация</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2" />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-900">
              {users.map((u) => {
                const self = u.id === me?.id;
                return (
                  <tr key={u.id} className={u.isBlocked ? 'opacity-50' : ''}>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <div>{u.username}{self && ' (вы)'}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{u.email}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <select className="input !w-auto !py-1" value={u.role} disabled={self} onChange={(e) => void patch(u.id, { role: e.target.value as 'ADMIN' | 'USER' })}>
                        <option value="USER">User</option>
                        <option value="ADMIN">Admin</option>
                      </select>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-400">
                        <input type="checkbox" checked={u.canUpload || u.role === 'ADMIN'} disabled={u.role === 'ADMIN'} onChange={(e) => void patch(u.id, { canUpload: e.target.checked })} />
                        <Upload size={14} />
                      </label>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-400">{formatDate(u.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">
                      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                        <button className="btn" disabled={self} onClick={() => void patch(u.id, { isBlocked: !u.isBlocked })}>
                          <Ban size={14} /> {u.isBlocked ? 'Разблокировать' : 'Блок'}
                        </button>
                        <button className="btn btn-danger" disabled={self} onClick={() => void remove(u)} aria-label="Удалить">
                          <Trash2 size={14} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------------- Страница ---------------- */

export default function AdminPage() {
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('stats');
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<Summary | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user?.role !== 'ADMIN') return;
    api<Summary>('/admin/summary').then(setSummary).catch(() => undefined);
  }, [user?.role, tab]);

  if (user?.role !== 'ADMIN') return <div className="card p-8 text-center text-sm text-zinc-400">Доступ только для администратора.</div>;

  const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string; badge?: number }[] = [
    { id: 'stats', label: 'Хранилище и сканирование' },
    { id: 'review', label: 'Нераспознанные', badge: summary?.needsReview },
    { id: 'users', label: 'Пользователи' },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Админ-панель</h1>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
        {tabs.map((t) => (
          <button key={t.id} onClick={() => setTab(t.id)} className={cn('flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-sm', tab === t.id ? 'bg-zinc-700 text-white' : 'bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white')}>
            {t.label}
            {!!t.badge && <span className="rounded-full bg-accent px-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-black">{t.badge}</span>}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {tab === 'stats' && <StatsTab />}
      {tab === 'review' && <ReviewTab />}
      {tab === 'users' && <UsersTab />}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: app\(app)\browse\page.tsx

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Check, Download, ExternalLink, Pause, Play, Youtube } from 'lucide-react';
import { Cover } from '@/components/Cover';
import { api, ApiError } from '@/lib/api';
import type { DiscoverMeta, DiscoverResponse, ExternalItem } from '@/lib/types';
import { cn, formatDuration, youtubeSearchUrl } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';
import { usePlayer, type NewQueueItem } from '@/store/player';

type Tab = 'chart' | 'new' | 'genre';

const TABS: { id: Tab; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'chart', label: 'Чарты стран' },
  { id: 'new', label: 'Новинки' },
  { id: 'genre', label: 'По жанрам' },
];

const TONES: Record<string, string> = {
  'hip-hop': 'from-amber-400 to-orange-800',
  rock: 'from-red-500 to-zinc-900',
  electronic: 'from-cyan-400 to-indigo-800',
  pop: 'from-pink-400 to-fuchsia-800',
  metal: 'from-zinc-500 to-black',
  'russian-rap': 'from-yellow-300 to-red-800',
  synthwave: 'from-fuchsia-500 to-violet-900',
  indie: 'from-lime-400 to-emerald-900',
  jazz: 'from-amber-200 to-yellow-800',
  rnb: 'from-rose-400 to-purple-900',
  dance: 'from-sky-400 to-blue-800',
  classical: 'from-stone-300 to-stone-700',
  country: 'from-orange-300 to-amber-800',
  reggae: 'from-green-400 to-yellow-700',
  punk: 'from-red-600 to-zinc-800',
  folk: 'from-emerald-300 to-teal-800',
};

const itemKey = (i: ExternalItem) => `${i.artist}|${i.title}`;

export default function BrowsePage() {
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const canDownload = user?.role === 'ADMIN' || Boolean(user?.canUpload);
  const [meta, setMeta] = useState<DiscoverMeta | null>(null);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('chart');
  const [country, setCountry] = useState('US');
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [data, setData] = useState<DiscoverResponse | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [hint, setHint] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const reqId = useRef(0);

  const current = usePlayer((s) => s.queue[s.index]);
  const playing = usePlayer((s) => s.playing);

  useEffect(() => {
    api<DiscoverMeta>('/discover/meta')
      .then(setMeta)
      .catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab === 'genre' && !genre) {
      setData(null);
      setError(null);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    const id = ++reqId.current;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    const path = tab === 'chart' ? '/discover/charts' : tab === 'new' ? '/discover/new' : `/discover/genre/${genre}`;
    api<DiscoverResponse>(path, { query: tab === 'genre' ? {} : { country } })
      .then((r) => id === reqId.current && setData(r))
      .catch((e: Error) => {
        if (id !== reqId.current) return;
        setData(null);
        setError(e.message);
      })
      .finally(() => id === reqId.current && setLoading(false));
  }, [tab, country, genre]);

  const patchItem = (item: ExternalItem, p: Partial<ExternalItem>) =>
    setData((d) => d && { ...d, items: d.items.map((i) => (itemKey(i) === itemKey(item) ? { ...i, ...p } : i)) });

  const play = async (item: ExternalItem) => {
    setHint(null);
    const player = usePlayer.getState();

    if (item.local) {
      const key = current?.kind === 'local' && current.id === item.local.trackId;
      if (key) return player.toggle();
      const q: NewQueueItem = {
        kind: 'local',
        id: item.local.trackId,
        title: item.title,
        artist: item.artist,
        coverUrl: item.local.coverUrl ?? item.coverUrl,
        duration: item.local.duration,
      };
      return player.playQueue([q]);
    }

    setBusy(itemKey(item));
    try {
      let previewUrl = item.previewUrl;
      let coverUrl = item.coverUrl;
      if (!previewUrl) {
        const r = await api<{ previewUrl: string | null; coverUrl: string | null }>('/discover/preview', {
          query: { artist: item.artist, title: item.title },
        });
        previewUrl = r.previewUrl ?? undefined;
        coverUrl ??= r.coverUrl ?? undefined;
      }
      if (previewUrl) {
        player.playQueue([{ kind: 'preview', title: item.title, artist: item.artist, coverUrl, src: previewUrl, duration: 30 }]);
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404)) setHint(`Превью недоступно: ${(e as Error).message}`);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
    setHint(`Превью для «${item.title}» нет — открыт поиск на YouTube.`);
    window.open(youtubeSearchUrl(item.artist, item.title), '_blank', 'noopener');
  };

  const download = async (item: ExternalItem) => {
    setHint(null);
    if (!canDownload) {
      setHint('Загрузка в медиатеку доступна администратору и пользователям с правом загрузки.');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(itemKey(item));
    try {
      const r = await api<{ status: 'PENDING' | 'PROCESSING' }>('/discover/download', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { artist: item.artist, title: item.title },
      });
      patchItem(item, { downloadStatus: r.status });
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === 'already_local') setHint('Этот трек уже есть в медиатеке — обновите страницу.');
      else setHint((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const genres = meta?.genres ?? [];
  const genreLabel = genres.find((g) => g.tag === genre)?.label ?? data?.label;
  const localCount = data?.items.filter((i) => i.local).length ?? 0;
  const sourceName = data?.source === 'lastfm' ? 'Last.fm' : 'Deezer';

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Обзор</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">
          Главные топы, новинки недели и подборки по жанрам. Треки из вашей медиатеки играют полностью, остальные — в режиме превью.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 p-1">
          {TABS.map((t) => (
            <button key={t.id} onClick={() => setTab(t.id)} className={cn('rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm', tab === t.id ? 'bg-zinc-700 text-white' : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white')}>
              {t.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {tab !== 'genre' && (
          <select className="input !w-auto" value={country} onChange={(e) => setCountry(e.target.value)} aria-label="Страна">
            {(meta?.countries ?? [{ code: 'US', name: 'США' }]).map((c) => (
              <option key={c.code} value={c.code}>
                {c.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        )}
      </div>

      {tab === 'genre' && !genre && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {genres.map((g) => (
            <button
              key={g.tag}
              onClick={() => setGenre(g.tag)}
              className={cn('flex h-28 flex-col justify-end rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br p-4 text-left text-black shadow-lg', TONES[g.tag] ?? 'from-zinc-400 to-zinc-700')}
            >
              <span className="text-lg font-semibold">{g.label}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-black/70">Топ {g.label}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'genre' && genre && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => setGenre(null)}>
            <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Все жанры
          </button>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Топ {genreLabel}</h2>
        </div>
      )}

      {data && (tab !== 'genre' || genre) && (
        <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
          {tab === 'chart' && (data.source === 'lastfm' ? `Топ-${data.items.length} за неделю · Last.fm` : `Мировой топ · Deezer`)}
          {tab === 'new' && `Новинки недели · ${sourceName}`}
          {tab === 'genre' && `Топ ${genreLabel} · ${sourceName}`}
          {' · '}в вашей медиатеке: {localCount} из {data.items.length}
        </p>
      )}
      {hint && <div className="rounded-lg bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-amber-200">{hint}</div>}
      {error && <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}

      {(tab !== 'genre' || genre) && (
        <div className={cn('card divide-y divide-zinc-900', loading && 'opacity-60')}>
          {loading && !data && <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>}
          {data?.items.map((item, i) => {
            const k = itemKey(item);
            const isCurrent = item.local
              ? current?.kind === 'local' && current.id === item.local.trackId
              : current?.kind === 'preview' && current.src === item.previewUrl;
            const queued = item.downloadStatus === 'PENDING' || item.downloadStatus === 'PROCESSING';
            return (
              <div key={`${k}-${i}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 hover:bg-zinc-900/60">
                <span className="w-6 text-right text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-600">{i + 1}</span>
                <button className="relative shrink-0" onClick={() => void play(item)} disabled={busy === k} aria-label="Играть">
                  <Cover url={item.local?.coverUrl ?? item.coverUrl} title={item.title} size={44} />
                  <span className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center rounded bg-black/50 opacity-0 transition-opacity hover:opacity-100">
                    {isCurrent && playing ? <Pause size={18} fill="white" /> : <Play size={18} fill="white" />}
                  </span>
                </button>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className={cn('truncate text-sm', isCurrent && 'text-accent')}>{item.title}</div>
                  <div className="truncate text-xs text-zinc-400">{item.artist}</div>
                </div>

                {item.local ? (
                  <span className="hidden shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-yellow-400/15 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-yellow-300 sm:flex">
                    <Check size={12} /> В медиатеке{item.local.duration ? ` · ${formatDuration(item.local.duration)}` : ''}
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <a
                      className="rounded p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-white"
                      href={youtubeSearchUrl(item.artist, item.title)}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      title="Искать на YouTube"
                    >
                      <Youtube size={16} />
                    </a>
                    {item.externalUrl && (
                      <a className="rounded p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-white" href={item.externalUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" title="Открыть в сервисе">
                        <ExternalLink size={16} />
                      </a>
                    )}
                    {queued ? (
                      <span className="shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-300">
                        {item.downloadStatus === 'PROCESSING' ? 'Скачивается' : 'В очереди'}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <button className="btn btn-primary shrink-0 !px-3 !py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={() => void download(item)} disabled={busy === k}>
                        <Download size={14} /> В медиатеку
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {data && data.items.length === 0 && <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Список пуст</div>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Play } from 'lucide-react';
import { TrackTable } from '@/components/TrackTable';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { Paged, Track } from '@/lib/types';
import { trackToItem, usePlayer } from '@/store/player';

export default function FavoritesPage() {
  const [tracks, setTracks] = useState<Track[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api<Paged<Track>>('/favorites', { query: { limit: 200 } })
      .then((r) => setTracks(r.items))
      .catch((e: Error) => setError(e.message));
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Избранное</h1>
        <button
          className="btn btn-primary"
          disabled={!tracks?.length}
          onClick={() => tracks && usePlayer.getState().playQueue(tracks.map(trackToItem), 0)}
        >
          <Play size={16} fill="currentColor" /> Играть
        </button>
      </div>
      {error && <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}
      {tracks && (
        <TrackTable
          tracks={tracks}
          onPlay={(i) => usePlayer.getState().playQueue(tracks.map(trackToItem), i)}
          onDelete={(id) => setTracks((ts) => ts && ts.filter((t) => t.id !== id))}
          // Снятая звезда не удаляет строку сразу: можно передумать до обновления страницы
          emptyText="Пока пусто. Нажмите ♥ у трека, чтобы добавить его сюда."
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================
FILE: app\(app)\import\page.tsx
====================
'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { FileText, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

/* ---------------- Типы ---------------- */

interface ImportTrack {
  artist: string;
  title: string;
}
interface ImportResult {
  playlist: { id: string; name: string };
  total: number;
  matched: number;
  missing: ImportTrack[];
}
interface SpPlaylist {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  total: number;
}

/* ---------------- Spotify: OAuth Authorization Code + PKCE (всё происходит в браузере) ---------------- */

const SCOPES = 'playlist-read-private playlist-read-collaborative';
const TOKEN_KEY = 'loral-sp-token';
const VERIFIER_KEY = 'loral-sp-verifier';
const STATE_KEY = 'loral-sp-state';
const PENDING_KEY = 'loral-pending-import';

const ALPHABET = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789-._~';

function randomString(len = 64): string {
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(len));
  return Array.from(bytes, (b) => ALPHABET[b % ALPHABET.length]).join('');
}

async function challengeFor(verifier: string): Promise<string> {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(verifier));
  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(digest)))
    .replace(/\+/g, '-')
    .replace(/\//g, '_')
    .replace(/=+$/, '');
}

class SpotifyError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public status: number,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

function loadToken(): string | null {
  try {
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const { token, exp } = JSON.parse(raw) as { token: string; exp: number };
    return Date.now() < exp ? token : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function spotify<T>(token: string, urlOrPath: string): Promise<T> {
  const url = urlOrPath.startsWith('http') ? urlOrPath : `https://api.spotify.com/v1${urlOrPath}`;
  const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (res.ok) return (await res.json()) as T;
  const body = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as { error?: { message?: string } } | null;
  throw new SpotifyError(res.status, body?.error?.message ?? `Ошибка Spotify ${res.status}`);
}

interface Paging<T> {
  items: T[];
  next: string | null;
}

async function fetchAll<T>(token: string, firstUrl: string, limit = 5000): Promise<T[]> {
  const out: T[] = [];
  let next: string | null = firstUrl;
  while (next && out.length < limit) {
    const page: Paging<T> = await spotify<Paging<T>>(token, next);
    out.push(...page.items);
    next = page.next;
  }
  return out;
}

interface RawPlaylist {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  // Spotify переименовал tracks → items (февраль 2026); поддерживаем оба варианта
  items?: { total?: number };
  tracks?: { total?: number };
}
interface RawEntry {
  item?: RawTrack | null;
  track?: RawTrack | null;
}
interface RawTrack {
  name?: string;
  type?: string;
  artists?: { name: string }[];
}

async function fetchPlaylistTracks(token: string, id: string): Promise<ImportTrack[]> {
  let entries: RawEntry[];
  try {
    entries = await fetchAll<RawEntry>(token, `/playlists/${id}/items?limit=100&additional_types=track`);
  } catch (e) {
    if (!(e instanceof SpotifyError) || e.status !== 404) throw e;
    entries = await fetchAll<RawEntry>(token, `/playlists/${id}/tracks?limit=100`); // старое название эндпоинта
  }
  const out: ImportTrack[] = [];
  for (const e of entries) {
    const t = e.item ?? e.track;
    if (!t?.name || (t.type && t.type !== 'track')) continue; // пропускаем подкасты и пустые записи
    const artist = t.artists?.[0]?.name;
    if (artist) out.push({ artist, title: t.name });
  }
  return out;
}

/* ---------------- Страница ---------------- */

export default function ImportPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'spotify' | 'file'>('spotify');
  const [clientId, setClientId] = useState<string | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [redirectUri, setRedirectUri] = useState('');
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [playlists, setPlaylists] = useState<SpPlaylist[] | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // файл / текст
  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState('');
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [plName, setPlName] = useState('');

  const isLocalhost = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.location.hostname === 'localhost';

  const logoutSpotify = useCallback(() => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    setToken(null);
    setPlaylists(null);
  }, []);

  const loadPlaylists = useCallback(
    async (tk: string) => {
      setBusy('Загружаю ваши плейлисты из Spotify…');
      try {
        const raw = await fetchAll<RawPlaylist>(tk, '/me/playlists?limit=50');
        setPlaylists(raw.filter(Boolean).map((p) => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name, total: p.items?.total ?? p.tracks?.total ?? 0 })));
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 401) {
          logoutSpotify();
          setError('Сессия Spotify истекла. Войдите снова.');
        } else if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 403) {
          setError(
            `Spotify отказал в доступе (${e.message}). Приложение в режиме разработки доступно только добавленным пользователям — добавьте свой аккаунт в User Management в панели разработчика Spotify.`,
          );
        } else setError((e as Error).message);
      } finally {
        setBusy(null);
      }
    },
    [logoutSpotify],
  );

  // Инициализация: настройки сервера, возврат из Spotify с ?code=…
  useEffect(() => {
    setRedirectUri(`${window.location.origin}/import`);
    let cancelled = false;

    (async () => {
      const cfg = await api<{ spotifyClientId: string | null }>('/import/config').catch(() => ({ spotifyClientId: null }));
      if (cancelled) return;
      setClientId(cfg.spotifyClientId);

      // параметры возврата: из адресной строки или сохранённые, если пользователь успел залогиниться в Loral
      const search = sessionStorage.getItem(PENDING_KEY) ?? window.location.search;
      sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_KEY);
      const params = new URLSearchParams(search);
      const code = params.get('code');
      const spError = params.get('error');
      if (code || spError) window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/import');

      if (spError) {
        setError(spError === 'access_denied' ? 'Вы отклонили доступ в Spotify.' : `Spotify: ${spError}`);
        return;
      }
      if (code && cfg.spotifyClientId) {
        setBusy('Завершаю вход в Spotify…');
        try {
          if (params.get('state') !== sessionStorage.getItem(STATE_KEY)) throw new Error('Не совпал параметр state — начните вход заново.');
          const verifier = sessionStorage.getItem(VERIFIER_KEY);
          if (!verifier) throw new Error('Потерян code_verifier — начните вход заново.');
          const res = await fetch('https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
            body: new URLSearchParams({
              client_id: cfg.spotifyClientId,
              grant_type: 'authorization_code',
              code,
              redirect_uri: `${window.location.origin}/import`,
              code_verifier: verifier,
            }),
          });
          const data = (await res.json()) as { access_token?: string; expires_in?: number; error_description?: string };
          if (!res.ok || !data.access_token) throw new Error(data.error_description ?? 'Spotify не выдал токен');
          sessionStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, JSON.stringify({ token: data.access_token, exp: Date.now() + (data.expires_in ?? 3600) * 1000 - 30_000 }));
          sessionStorage.removeItem(VERIFIER_KEY);
          sessionStorage.removeItem(STATE_KEY);
          if (!cancelled) {
            setToken(data.access_token);
            await loadPlaylists(data.access_token);
          }
        } catch (e) {
          if (!cancelled) setError((e as Error).message);
        } finally {
          if (!cancelled) setBusy(null);
        }
        return;
      }

      const saved = loadToken();
      if (saved) {
        setToken(saved);
        void loadPlaylists(saved);
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [loadPlaylists]);

  const connect = async () => {
    if (!clientId) return;
    setError(null);
    const verifier = randomString(64);
    const state = randomString(16);
    sessionStorage.setItem(VERIFIER_KEY, verifier);
    sessionStorage.setItem(STATE_KEY, state);
    const url = new URL('https://accounts.spotify.com/authorize');
    url.search = new URLSearchParams({
      client_id: clientId,
      response_type: 'code',
      redirect_uri: redirectUri,
      scope: SCOPES,
      state,
      code_challenge_method: 'S256',
      code_challenge: await challengeFor(verifier),
    }).toString();
    window.location.href = url.toString();
  };

  const importFromSpotify = async (p: SpPlaylist) => {
    if (!token) return;
    setError(null);
    setBusy(`Читаю треки «${p.name}»…`);
    try {
      const tracks = await fetchPlaylistTracks(token, p.id);
      if (tracks.length === 0) throw new Error('В плейлисте не нашлось треков.');
      setBusy('Сопоставляю с вашей медиатекой…');
      const created = await api<ImportResult>('/import/playlist', { method: 'POST', body: { name: p.name, tracks } });
      router.push(`/playlists/${created.playlist.id}`);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 401) {
        logoutSpotify();
        setError('Сессия Spotify истекла. Войдите снова.');
      } else if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 403) {
        setError('Spotify не отдаёт содержимое этого плейлиста: доступны только плейлисты, которые вы создали или ведёте совместно. Для остальных используйте импорт из CSV (вкладка «Файл или текст»).');
      } else if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 429) {
        setError('Spotify ограничил число запросов. Подождите минуту и повторите.');
      } else setError((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const onFile = async (f: File | undefined) => {
    if (!f) return;
    setFileName(f.name);
    setText(await f.text());
    if (!plName) setPlName(f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ''));
  };

  const importFromText = async () => {
    setError(null);
    setBusy('Импортирую…');
    try {
      const created = await api<ImportResult>('/import/playlist', { method: 'POST', body: { name: plName.trim() || 'Импортированный плейлист', text } });
      router.push(`/playlists/${created.playlist.id}`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Импорт плейлиста</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">
          Loral создаст плейлист сразу. Треки, которых нет в медиатеке, скачиваются в фоне и появляются в плейлисте по мере готовности.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 p-1 text-sm w-fit">
        {(
          [
            ['spotify', 'Spotify'],
            ['file', 'Файл или текст'],
          ] as const
        ).map(([id, label]) => (
          <button key={id} onClick={() => setTab(id)} className={cn('rounded-md px-4 py-1.5', tab === id ? 'bg-accent font-semibold text-black' : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white')}>
            {label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {error && <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}
      {busy && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">
          <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin text-accent" /> {busy}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'spotify' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {clientId === undefined && <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>}

          {clientId === null && (
            <div className="card space-y-3 p-5 text-sm text-zinc-300">
              <div className="font-semibold text-white">Вход через Spotify ещё не настроен</div>
              <ol className="list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5">
                <li>
                  Откройте <b>developer.spotify.com/dashboard</b> и создайте приложение (потребуется аккаунт Spotify Premium).
                </li>
                <li>
                  В поле <b>Redirect URI</b> укажите: <code className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-yellow-300">{redirectUri || 'http://127.0.0.1:3000/import'}</code>
                </li>
                <li>
                  Скопируйте <b>Client ID</b> в <code className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5">backend/.env</code> (<code>SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID=…</code>) и перезапустите сервер.
                </li>
              </ol>
              <p className="text-zinc-500">Без настройки можно импортировать плейлист из CSV-файла — вкладка «Файл или текст».</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {clientId && !token && !busy && (
            <div className="card space-y-4 p-5">
              {isLocalhost && (
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-200">
                  Spotify не принимает адрес <b>localhost</b>. Откройте Loral как <b>http://127.0.0.1:3000</b> (войдите там заново) и повторите.
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="text-sm text-zinc-300">
                Loral запросит у Spotify доступ только на чтение ваших плейлистов. Токен хранится в браузере до закрытия вкладки и на сервер не отправляется.
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                Redirect URI в настройках вашего Spotify-приложения должен быть: <code className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-yellow-300">{redirectUri}</code>
              </div>
              <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={connect} disabled={isLocalhost}>
                Войти через Spotify
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {token && playlists && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                <span className="text-zinc-400">Ваши плейлисты в Spotify ({playlists.length})</span>
                <button className="text-zinc-500 hover:text-white" onClick={logoutSpotify}>
                  Выйти из Spotify
                </button>
              </div>
              {playlists.length === 0 && <div className="card p-6 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Плейлистов не найдено.</div>}
              <ul className="card divide-y divide-white/5">
                {playlists.map((p) => (
                  <li key={p.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="truncate text-sm">{p.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{p.total} треков</div>
                    </div>
                    <button className="btn shrink-0" onClick={() => void importFromSpotify(p)} disabled={!!busy}>
                      Перенести
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'file' && (
        <div className="card space-y-4 p-5">
          <div className="space-y-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-white">
              <FileText size={16} className="text-accent" /> Как получить список из Spotify без входа
            </div>
            <ol className="list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5">
              <li>
                Откройте <b>exportify.net</b>, войдите через Spotify и нажмите «Export» у нужного плейлиста — скачается CSV.
              </li>
              <li>Загрузите этот файл сюда. Подойдёт и обычный текстовый список, по строке на трек: «Исполнитель - Название».</li>
            </ol>
          </div>

          <input className="input" placeholder="Название плейлиста" value={plName} onChange={(e) => setPlName(e.target.value)} maxLength={100} />
          <label className="btn cursor-pointer">
            <FileText size={14} /> {fileName || 'Выбрать файл (.csv, .txt)'}
            <input type="file" accept=".csv,.txt,text/csv,text/plain" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => void onFile(e.target.files?.[0])} />
          </label>
          <textarea
            className="input min-h-40 font-mono text-xs"
            placeholder={'или вставьте список:\nLogic - 44 Bars\nJay-Z - 99 Problems'}
            value={text}
            onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={importFromText} disabled={!text.trim() || !!busy}>
            {busy ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
            Импортировать
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================
FILE: app\(app)\library\page.tsx
====================
'use client';

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Play, Search, Shuffle, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { TrackTable } from '@/components/TrackTable';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { FiltersMeta, Paged, Track } from '@/lib/types';
import { dayEndISO, dayStartISO, useDebounced } from '@/lib/utils';
import { trackToItem, usePlayer } from '@/store/player';

const PAGE = 50;

const SORTS = [
  { v: 'added', l: 'Недавно добавленные' },
  { v: 'artist', l: 'Исполнитель' },
  { v: 'title', l: 'Название' },
  { v: 'year', l: 'Год' },
  { v: 'duration', l: 'Длительность' },
];

export default function LibraryPage() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState('');
  const [yearFrom, setYearFrom] = useState('');
  const [yearTo, setYearTo] = useState('');
  const [addedFrom, setAddedFrom] = useState('');
  const [addedTo, setAddedTo] = useState('');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState('added');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FiltersMeta | null>(null);
  const [data, setData] = useState<Paged<Track> | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const dq = useDebounced(q, 300);
  const reqId = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    api<FiltersMeta>('/filters')
      .then(setFilters)
      .catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  // при смене любого фильтра возвращаемся на первую страницу
  useEffect(() => setPage(1), [dq, genre, yearFrom, yearTo, addedFrom, addedTo, sort]);

  const query = useMemo(
    () => ({
      q: dq,
      genre,
      yearFrom,
      yearTo,
      addedFrom: addedFrom ? dayStartISO(addedFrom) : '',
      addedTo: addedTo ? dayEndISO(addedTo) : '',
      sort,
      page,
      limit: PAGE,
    }),
    [dq, genre, yearFrom, yearTo, addedFrom, addedTo, sort, page],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = ++reqId.current;
    setLoading(true);
    api<Paged<Track>>('/tracks', { query })
      .then((r) => {
        if (id !== reqId.current) return; // устаревший ответ
        setData(r);
        setError(null);
      })
      .catch((e: Error) => id === reqId.current && setError(e.message))
      .finally(() => id === reqId.current && setLoading(false));
  }, [query]);

  const hasFilters = Boolean(q || genre || yearFrom || yearTo || addedFrom || addedTo);
  const reset = () => {
    setQ('');
    setGenre('');
    setYearFrom('');
    setYearTo('');
    setAddedFrom('');
    setAddedTo('');
  };

  const playAt = useCallback(
    (i: number) => {
      if (data) usePlayer.getState().playQueue(data.items.map(trackToItem), i);
    },
    [data],
  );

  const playAll = (shuffle: boolean) => {
    if (!data?.items.length) return;
    const s = usePlayer.getState();
    if (s.shuffle !== shuffle) s.toggleShuffle();
    s.playQueue(data.items.map(trackToItem), shuffle ? Math.floor(Math.random() * data.items.length) : 0);
  };

  const pages = data ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.total / PAGE)) : 1;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Библиотека</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">{data ? `${data.total} треков` : ' '}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={() => playAll(false)} disabled={!data?.items.length}>
            <Play size={16} fill="currentColor" /> Играть
          </button>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => playAll(true)} disabled={!data?.items.length}>
            <Shuffle size={16} /> Вперемешку
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="card space-y-3 p-3">
        <div className="relative">
          <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-zinc-500" />
          <input
            className="input !pl-9"
            placeholder="Поиск по исполнителю и названию…"
            value={q}
            onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 md:grid-cols-6">
          <select className="input col-span-2 md:col-span-1" value={genre} onChange={(e) => setGenre(e.target.value)} aria-label="Жанр">
            <option value="">Все жанры</option>
            {filters?.genres.map((g) => (
              <option key={g.name} value={g.name}>
                {g.name} ({g.count})
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <input
            className="input"
            type="number"
            placeholder={`Год от${filters?.years.min ? ` (${filters.years.min})` : ''}`}
            value={yearFrom}
            onChange={(e) => setYearFrom(e.target.value)}
          />
          <input
            className="input"
            type="number"
            placeholder={`Год до${filters?.years.max ? ` (${filters.years.max})` : ''}`}
            value={yearTo}
            onChange={(e) => setYearTo(e.target.value)}
          />
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500">
            Добавлено с
            <input className="input" type="date" value={addedFrom} onChange={(e) => setAddedFrom(e.target.value)} />
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-500">
            по
            <input className="input" type="date" value={addedTo} onChange={(e) => setAddedTo(e.target.value)} />
          </label>
          <select className="input" value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)} aria-label="Сортировка">
            {SORTS.map((s) => (
              <option key={s.v} value={s.v}>
                {s.l}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        {hasFilters && (
          <button className="btn" onClick={reset}>
            <X size={14} /> Сбросить фильтры
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {error && <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}

      <div className={loading && data ? 'opacity-60 transition-opacity' : ''}>
        {data ? (
          <TrackTable
            tracks={data.items}
            onPlay={playAt}
            onDelete={(id) => setData((d) => d && { ...d, total: d.total - 1, items: d.items.filter((t) => t.id !== id) })}
            emptyText={hasFilters ? 'По этим условиям ничего не найдено' : 'Ваша медиатека пока пуста. Загрузите треки на странице «Загрузка» или перенесите плейлист из Spotify.'}
          />
        ) : (
          !error && <div className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>
        )}
      </div>

      {pages > 1 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
          <button className="btn" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}>
            Назад
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
            {page} / {pages}
          </span>
          <button className="btn" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}>
            Вперёд
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================
FILE: app\(app)\playlists\[id]\page.tsx
====================
'use client';

import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Play, RotateCcw, Trash2, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { TrackTable } from '@/components/TrackTable';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { PlaylistDetail, PlaylistDownload } from '@/lib/types';
import { trackToItem, usePlayer } from '@/store/player';

const DOWNLOAD_LABEL: Record<PlaylistDownload['status'], string> = {
  PENDING: 'В очереди',
  PROCESSING: 'В процессе загрузки...',
  COMPLETED: 'Готово',
  FAILED: 'Не найдено',
  CANCELLED: 'Отменено',
};

export default function PlaylistPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pl, setPl] = useState<PlaylistDetail | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [rev, setRev] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    let sawPlaylist = false;
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

    const load = async () => {
      try {
        const data = await api<PlaylistDetail>(`/playlists/${id}`);
        if (cancelled) return;
        sawPlaylist = true;
        setError(null);
        setPl(data);
        const active = (data.downloads ?? []).some((d) => d.status === 'PENDING' || d.status === 'PROCESSING');
        const processing = (data.downloads ?? []).some((d) => d.status === 'PROCESSING');
        if (active) timer = setTimeout(() => void load(), processing ? 2000 : 4000);
      } catch (e) {
        if (!cancelled && !sawPlaylist) setError((e as Error).message);
      }
    };

    void load();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [id, rev]);

  if (error) return <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>;
  if (!pl) return <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>;

  const patch = async (body: { name?: string; isPublic?: boolean }) => {
    await api(`/playlists/${pl.id}`, { method: 'PATCH', body });
    setPl({ ...pl, ...body });
  };

  const removeTrack = async (trackId: string) => {
    await api(`/playlists/${pl.id}/tracks/${trackId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    setPl({ ...pl, tracks: pl.tracks.filter((t) => t.id !== trackId) });
  };

  const remove = async () => {
    if (!confirm(`Удалить плейлист «${pl.name}»?`)) return;
    await api(`/playlists/${pl.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    router.replace('/playlists');
  };

  const downloads = pl.downloads ?? [];
  const active = downloads.some((d) => d.status === 'PENDING' || d.status === 'PROCESSING');
  const failed = downloads.filter((d) => d.status === 'FAILED');
  const shown = active ? downloads.filter((d) => d.status !== 'COMPLETED') : downloads.filter((d) => d.status === 'FAILED' || d.status === 'CANCELLED');

  const patchDownload = (id: string, patch: Partial<PlaylistDownload>) =>
    setPl({ ...pl, downloads: downloads.map((d) => (d.id === id ? { ...d, ...patch } : d)) });

  const cancelDownload = async (downloadId: string) => {
    await api(`/playlists/${pl.id}/downloads/${downloadId}/cancel`, { method: 'POST' });
    patchDownload(downloadId, { status: 'CANCELLED', error: 'Отменено', progress: null });
  };

  const retryDownload = async (downloadId: string) => {
    await api(`/playlists/${pl.id}/downloads/${downloadId}/retry`, { method: 'POST' });
    patchDownload(downloadId, { status: 'PENDING', error: null, progress: null });
    setRev((n) => n + 1);
  };

  const retryAll = async () => {
    await api(`/playlists/${pl.id}/downloads/retry`, { method: 'POST' });
    setPl({
      ...pl,
      downloads: downloads.map((d) => (d.status === 'FAILED' ? { ...d, status: 'PENDING', error: null, progress: null } : d)),
    });
    setRev((n) => n + 1);
  };

  const dismissDownload = async (downloadId: string) => {
    await api(`/downloads/errors/${downloadId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    setPl({ ...pl, downloads: downloads.filter((d) => d.id !== downloadId) });
  };

  const dismissAll = async () => {
    await api('/downloads/errors', { method: 'DELETE' });
    setPl({ ...pl, downloads: downloads.filter((d) => d.status !== 'FAILED') });
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          {pl.isMine ? (
            <input
              className="w-full bg-transparent text-2xl font-bold outline-none focus:underline"
              defaultValue={pl.name}
              maxLength={100}
              onBlur={(e) => {
                const v = e.target.value.trim();
                if (v && v !== pl.name) void patch({ name: v });
                else e.target.value = pl.name;
              }}
            />
          ) : (
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">{pl.name}</h1>
          )}
          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">
            {pl.tracks.length} треков · {pl.owner}
            {active ? ` · загружается ${downloads.filter((d) => d.status === 'PENDING' || d.status === 'PROCESSING').length}` : ''}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {pl.isMine && failed.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <button className="btn" onClick={() => void retryAll()}>
                <RotateCcw size={14} /> Повторить ошибки
              </button>
              <button className="btn" onClick={() => void dismissAll()}>
                <Trash2 size={14} /> Очистить все
              </button>
            </>
          )}
          {pl.isMine && (
            <button className="btn btn-danger" onClick={remove}>
              <Trash2 size={14} /> Удалить
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            className="btn btn-primary"
            disabled={!pl.tracks.length}
            onClick={() => usePlayer.getState().playQueue(pl.tracks.map(trackToItem), 0)}
          >
            <Play size={16} fill="currentColor" /> Играть
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {shown.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="card divide-y divide-white/5">
          {shown.map((d) => (
            <li key={d.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="truncate">
                  {d.artist} — {d.title}
                </div>
                {d.status === 'PROCESSING' && (
                  <div className="mt-1.5 h-1 overflow-hidden rounded bg-zinc-800">
                    <div className="h-full bg-accent transition-all" style={{ width: `${d.progress ?? 0}%` }} />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <span
                className={
                  d.status === 'FAILED'
                    ? 'block max-w-64 truncate text-red-300'
                    : d.status === 'CANCELLED'
                      ? 'shrink-0 text-zinc-500'
                      : d.status === 'COMPLETED'
                        ? 'shrink-0 text-emerald-300'
                        : 'shrink-0 text-zinc-400'
                }
                title={d.error ?? undefined}
              >
                {d.status === 'PROCESSING' && d.progress != null ? `${d.progress}%` : d.status === 'FAILED' ? d.error || DOWNLOAD_LABEL.FAILED : DOWNLOAD_LABEL[d.status]}
              </span>
              {pl.isMine && (d.status === 'PENDING' || d.status === 'PROCESSING') && (
                <button className="btn shrink-0 !px-2 !py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => void cancelDownload(d.id)}>
                  <X size={14} /> Отменить
                </button>
              )}
              {pl.isMine && d.status === 'FAILED' && (
                <>
                  <button className="btn shrink-0 !px-2 !py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => void retryDownload(d.id)}>
                    <RotateCcw size={14} /> Повторить
                  </button>
                  <button className="btn shrink-0 !px-2 !py-1 text-xs" title="Скрыть" onClick={() => void dismissDownload(d.id)}>
                    <X size={14} />
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}

      <TrackTable
        tracks={pl.tracks}
        onPlay={(i) => usePlayer.getState().playQueue(pl.tracks.map(trackToItem), i)}
        emptyText="Плейлист пуст. Добавляйте треки кнопкой «В плейлист» в библиотеке."
        renderExtra={
          pl.isMine
            ? (t) => (
                <button className="rounded p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-red-400" onClick={() => void removeTrack(t.id)} title="Убрать из плейлиста">
                  <X size={16} />
                </button>
              )
            : undefined
        }
      />
    </div>
  );
}


====================
FILE: app\(app)\playlists\page.tsx
====================
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Import, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { Cover } from '@/components/Cover';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { PlaylistSummary } from '@/lib/types';

export default function PlaylistsPage() {
  const [mine, setMine] = useState<PlaylistSummary[] | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = () => {
    api<{ items: PlaylistSummary[] }>('/playlists')
      .then((r) => setMine(r.items))
      .catch((e: Error) => setError(e.message));
  };
  useEffect(load, []);

  const create = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim()) return;
    try {
      await api('/playlists', { method: 'POST', body: { name: name.trim() } });
      setName('');
      load();
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Плейлисты</h1>
        <Link href="/import" className="btn">
          <Import size={16} /> Импорт из Spotify
        </Link>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={create} className="card flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-3">
        <input className="input !w-auto min-w-0 flex-1" placeholder="Название нового плейлиста" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} maxLength={100} />
        <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={!name.trim()}>
          <Plus size={16} /> Создать
        </button>
      </form>
      {error && <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}

      {mine === null ? (
        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>
      ) : mine.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Пока нет плейлистов.</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5">
          {mine.map((p) => (
            <Link key={p.id} href={`/playlists/${p.id}`} className="card block p-3 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-900">
              <Cover url={p.coverUrl} title={p.name} size={160} className="!h-auto aspect-square !w-full" />
              <div className="mt-3 truncate text-sm font-medium">{p.name}</div>
              <div className="truncate text-xs text-zinc-500">{p.trackCount} треков</div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================
FILE: app\(app)\upload\page.tsx
====================
'use client';

import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Loader2, UploadCloud } from 'lucide-react';
import { api, ApiError, uploadFile } from '@/lib/api';
import { NAMING_HINT, validateUploadName } from '@/lib/filename';
import { cn, formatBytes } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';

interface Row {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  size: number;
  state: 'invalid' | 'queued' | 'uploading' | 'done' | 'error';
  progress: number;
  message?: string;
}

let rowId = 0;

export default function UploadPage() {
  const canUpload = useAuth((s) => s.user?.canUpload);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>([]);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const running = useRef(false);
  const queue = useRef<{ id: number; file: File }[]>([]);
  const playlistIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const patch = (id: number, p: Partial<Row>) =>
    setRows((rs) => rs.map((r) => (r.id === id ? { ...r, ...p } : r)));

  const ensurePlaylist = async () => {
    if (playlistIdRef.current) return playlistIdRef.current;
    const now = new Date();
    const dateStr = `${now.getDate()}.${now.getMonth() + 1}.${now.getFullYear()} ${now.getHours()}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    try {
      const pl = await api<{ id: string }>('/playlists', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: {
          name: `Загрузка от ${dateStr}`,
          isPublic: false,
        },
      });
      playlistIdRef.current = pl.id;
      return pl.id;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  };

  const pump = async () => {
    if (running.current) return;
    running.current = true;

    // Создаем плейлист перед стартом пачки файлов
    const playlistId = await ensurePlaylist();

    while (queue.current.length) {
      const { id, file } = queue.current.shift()!;
      patch(id, { state: 'uploading', progress: 0 });
      try {
        const r = await uploadFile(file, (progress) => patch(id, { progress }));
        const createdTrack = (r as { track?: { id: string } }).track;

        if (r.status === 'created' && createdTrack?.id) {
          if (playlistId) {
            await api(`/playlists/${playlistId}/tracks`, {
              method: 'POST',
              body: { trackId: createdTrack.id },
            }).catch(() => null);
          }
          patch(id, { state: 'done', progress: 100, message: 'Добавлено в плейлист и библиотеку' });
        } else if (r.status === 'duplicate') {
          patch(id, { state: 'invalid', message: r.error ?? 'Уже есть в библиотеке' });
        } else {
          patch(id, { state: 'error', message: r.error ?? 'Не удалось добавить' });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        patch(id, { state: 'error', message: e instanceof ApiError ? e.message : 'Ошибка загрузки' });
      }
    }

    // Сбрасываем ID плейлиста для следующей сессии загрузки
    playlistIdRef.current = null;
    running.current = false;
  };

  const addFiles = (list: FileList | File[]) => {
    const fresh: Row[] = [];
    for (const file of Array.from(list)) {
      const id = ++rowId;
      const check = validateUploadName(file.name);
      if (check.ok) {
        fresh.push({ id, name: file.name, size: file.size, state: 'queued', progress: 0 });
        queue.current.push({ id, file });
      } else {
        fresh.push({ id, name: file.name, size: file.size, state: 'invalid', progress: 0, message: check.error });
      }
    }
    setRows((rs) => [...fresh, ...rs]);
    void pump();
  };

  if (!canUpload) {
    return <div className="card p-8 text-center text-sm text-pink-300/70">У вас нет прав на загрузку треков. Обратитесь к администратору.</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-300 via-rose-300 to-purple-300 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
        Загрузка треков
      </h1>

      <div
        onDragOver={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          setDragging(true);
        }}
        onDragLeave={() => setDragging(false)}
        onDrop={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          setDragging(false);
          addFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);
        }}
        onClick={() => input.current?.click()}
        className={cn(
          'flex cursor-pointer flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed px-6 py-14 text-center transition-all duration-300 backdrop-blur-md',
          dragging
            ? 'border-pink-400 bg-pink-500/10 scale-[1.01]'
            : 'border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/20 hover:border-pink-400/50 hover:bg-pink-900/20',
        )}
      >
        <UploadCloud size={40} className="text-pink-400 animate-pulse" />
        <div className="font-semibold text-pink-100">Перетащите файлы сюда или нажмите для выбора</div>
        <div className="text-xs text-pink-300/60">
          Формат имени: <code className="rounded-md bg-pink-950/60 px-2 py-0.5 border border-pink-500/20 text-pink-200">Исполнитель - Название.mp3</code>
        </div>
        <input
          ref={input}
          type="file"
          multiple
          accept=".mp3,.flac,.wav,.ogg,.m4a,.opus,audio/*"
          className="hidden"
          onChange={(e) => {
            if (e.target.files) addFiles(e.target.files);
            e.target.value = '';
          }}
        />
      </div>

      {rows.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/20 backdrop-blur-md divide-y divide-pink-500/10 overflow-hidden">
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3">
              {r.state === 'done' && <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="shrink-0 text-pink-400" />}
              {(r.state === 'invalid' || r.state === 'error') && <AlertCircle size={18} className="shrink-0 text-rose-400" />}
              {r.state === 'uploading' && <Loader2 size={18} className="shrink-0 animate-spin text-pink-300" />}
              {r.state === 'queued' && <Loader2 size={18} className="shrink-0 text-pink-500/40" />}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="truncate text-sm font-medium text-pink-100">{r.name}</div>
                <div className={cn('text-xs', r.state === 'invalid' || r.state === 'error' ? 'text-rose-300' : 'text-pink-300/60')}>
                  {r.state === 'uploading'
                    ? `Загрузка… ${r.progress}%`
                    : r.state === 'queued'
                      ? `В очереди · ${formatBytes(r.size)}`
                      : (r.message ?? '')}
                </div>
                {r.state === 'uploading' && (
                  <div className="mt-1.5 h-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-pink-950">
                    <div className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-400 transition-all duration-200" style={{ width: `${r.progress}%` }} />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <p className="text-xs text-pink-400/40">{NAMING_HINT}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

====================
FILE: app\(app)\layout.tsx
====================
'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { GlobalPlayer } from '@/components/GlobalPlayer';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/Sidebar';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { User } from '@/lib/types';
import { useAuth, useAuthHydrated } from '@/store/auth';

/**
 * Этот layout не перемонтируется при навигации между страницами группы (app),
 * поэтому <GlobalPlayer/> с единственным <audio> живёт непрерывно — музыка не прерывается.
 */
export default function AppLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const hydrated = useAuthHydrated();
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hydrated && !user) {
      // Spotify вернул пользователя на /import до входа — запоминаем код авторизации
      if (location.pathname === '/import' && location.search.includes('code=')) {
        sessionStorage.setItem('loral-pending-import', location.search);
      }
      router.replace('/login');
    }
  }, [hydrated, user, router]);

  // Актуализируем роль/права (их мог изменить админ)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    api<{ user: User }>('/auth/me')
      .then((r) => useAuth.getState().setUser(r.user))
      .catch(() => undefined);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [user?.id]);

  if (!hydrated || !user) {
    return <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen flex-col md:flex-row">
      <Sidebar />
      <main className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto pb-[104px]">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl p-4 md:p-8">{children}</div>
      </main>
      <GlobalPlayer />
    </div>
  );
}
