'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ListMusic, Pause, Play, Repeat, Repeat1, Shuffle, SkipBack, SkipForward, Trash2, Volume2, VolumeX, X, Maximize2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { assetUrl, refreshStreamToken, streamUrl } from '@/lib/api';
import { cn, formatDuration } from '@/lib/utils';
import { audioRef, usePlayer } from '@/store/player';
import { Cover } from './Cover';
import { FullScreenPlayer } from './FullScreenPlayer'; // Импортируем наш полноэкранный плеер

function onPlayError(e: unknown) {
  if ((e as Error)?.name !== 'AbortError') usePlayer.getState()._setPlaying(false);
}

export function GlobalPlayer() {
  const queue = usePlayer((s) => s.queue);
  const index = usePlayer((s) => s.index);
  const playing = usePlayer((s) => s.playing);
  const shuffle = usePlayer((s) => s.shuffle);
  const repeat = usePlayer((s) => s.repeat);
  const volume = usePlayer((s) => s.volume);
  const muted = usePlayer((s) => s.muted);
  const currentTime = usePlayer((s) => s.currentTime);
  const duration = usePlayer((s) => s.duration);
  const queueOpen = usePlayer((s) => s.queueOpen);
  const error = usePlayer((s) => s.error);
  const p = usePlayer.getState;

  const current = queue[index];
  const switching = useRef(false);
  const retried = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const [scrub, setScrub] = useState<number | null>(null);
  
  // Состояние для открытия полноэкранного плеера
  const [fullScreenOpen, setFullScreenOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (!a) return;
    if (!current) {
      a.pause();
      a.removeAttribute('src');
      a.load();
      return;
    }
    switching.current = true;
    a.src = current.kind === 'local' ? streamUrl(current.id!) : current.src!;
    if (usePlayer.getState().playing) a.play().catch(onPlayError);
  }, [current?.key]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (!a || !current) return;
    if (playing && a.paused) a.play().catch(onPlayError);
    else if (!playing && !a.paused) a.pause();
  }, [playing]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const a = audioRef.current;
    if (a) {
      a.volume = volume;
      a.muted = muted;
    }
  }, [volume, muted]);

  const dur = duration || current?.duration || 0;
  const shown = scrub ?? currentTime;
  const commitScrub = () => {
    if (scrub !== null) {
      p().seek(scrub);
      setScrub(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <audio
        ref={(el) => {
          audioRef.current = el;
        }}
        preload="auto"
        onTimeUpdate={(e) => p()._tick(e.currentTarget.currentTime, e.currentTarget.duration)}
        onLoadedMetadata={(e) => p()._tick(e.currentTarget.currentTime, e.currentTarget.duration)}
        onPlaying={() => {
          switching.current = false;
          p()._setPlaying(true);
          if (usePlayer.getState().error) p()._setError(null);
        }}
        onPlay={() => p()._setPlaying(true)}
        onPause={(e) => {
          if (!e.currentTarget.ended && !switching.current) p()._setPlaying(false);
        }}
        onEnded={() => p().next(true)}
      />

      {/* Очередь воспроизведения (выпадающая панель) */}
      {queueOpen && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-[96px] right-4 z-40 flex max-h-[60vh] w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md flex-col rounded-3xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/90 p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-2xl">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-pink-500/10 pb-3">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-pink-100">Очередь воспроизведения · {queue.length}</h3>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button className="rounded-xl bg-pink-900/30 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-pink-300 hover:bg-pink-800/40" onClick={() => p().clear()}>
                Очистить
              </button>
              <button className="rounded-xl bg-pink-900/30 p-1.5 text-pink-300 hover:bg-pink-800/40" onClick={() => p().setQueueOpen(false)}>
                <X size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <ul className="overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-pink-500/5 py-2">
            {queue.map((item, i) => (
              <li
                key={item.key}
                className={cn('flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 transition-colors hover:bg-pink-900/30', i === index && 'bg-pink-500/20')}
                onClick={() => {
                  p().jumpTo(i);
                  const remaining = queue.slice(i);
                  p().playQueue(remaining, 0);
                }}
              >
                <Cover url={item.coverUrl} title={item.title} size={36} />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className={cn('truncate text-sm font-medium', i === index ? 'text-pink-200' : 'text-pink-100/80')}>{item.title}</div>
                  <div className="truncate text-xs text-pink-300/50">{item.artist}</div>
                </div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Нижняя панель плеера */}
      <div className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-30 h-[88px] border-t border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/80 backdrop-blur-2xl">
        {current ? (
          <div className="mx-auto grid h-full max-w-[1600px] grid-cols-[1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 px-4 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,2fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
              {/* Кликабельная обложка с иконкой развертывания */}
              <div 
                className="relative group cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-105 overflow-hidden rounded-xl shadow-lg" 
                onClick={() => setFullScreenOpen(true)}
                title="Развернуть во весь экран"
              >
                <Cover url={current.coverUrl} title={current.title} size={52} />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                  <Maximize2 size={18} className="text-white" />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="min-w-0 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => setFullScreenOpen(true)}>
                <div className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-pink-100 hover:underline">{current.title}</div>
                <div className="truncate text-xs text-pink-300/60">{current.artist}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <button
                  className={cn('hidden rounded-xl p-2 transition-colors sm:block', shuffle ? 'text-pink-300 bg-pink-500/20' : 'text-pink-300/50 hover:text-pink-200')}
                  onClick={() => p().toggleShuffle()}
                >
                  <Shuffle size={18} />
                </button>
                <button className="rounded-xl p-2 text-pink-200 hover:bg-pink-900/30 transition-colors" onClick={() => p().prev()}>
                  <SkipBack size={20} />
                </button>
                <button
                  className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-pink-500 via-rose-400 to-purple-400 text-pink-950 shadow-lg shadow-pink-500/30 transition-transform hover:scale-105 active:scale-95"
                  onClick={() => p().toggle()}
                  aria-label={playing ? 'Пауза' : 'Играть'}
                >
                  {playing ? <Pause size={20} fill="currentColor" /> : <Play size={20} fill="currentColor" className="ml-0.5" />}
                </button>
                <button className="rounded-xl p-2 text-pink-200 hover:bg-pink-900/30 transition-colors" onClick={() => p().next()}>
                  <SkipForward size={20} />
                </button>
                <button
                  className={cn('hidden rounded-xl p-2 transition-colors sm:block', repeat !== 'off' ? 'text-pink-300 bg-pink-500/20' : 'text-pink-300/50 hover:text-pink-200')}
                  onClick={() => p().cycleRepeat()}
                >
                  {repeat === 'one' ? <Repeat1 size={18} /> : <Repeat size={18} />}
                </button>
              </div>
              <div className="hidden w-full items-center gap-3 text-xs tabular-nums text-pink-300/60 md:flex">
                <span className="w-10 text-right">{formatDuration(shown)}</span>
                <input
                  type="range"
                  className="h-1.5 flex-1 cursor-pointer accent-pink-400 rounded-lg bg-pink-950/60"
                  min={0}
                  max={dur || 1}
                  step={0.5}
                  value={Math.min(shown, dur || 1)}
                  onChange={(e) => setScrub(Number(e.target.value))}
                  onPointerUp={commitScrub}
                  onKeyUp={commitScrub}
                  onBlur={commitScrub}
                />
                <span className="w-10">{formatDuration(dur)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="hidden items-center justify-end gap-3 md:flex">
              <button
                className={cn('rounded-xl p-2 transition-colors', queueOpen ? 'bg-pink-500/20 text-pink-200' : 'text-pink-300/60 hover:text-pink-200')}
                onClick={() => p().setQueueOpen(!queueOpen)}
                title="Очередь"
              >
                <ListMusic size={20} />
              </button>
              <button className="rounded-xl p-2 text-pink-300/60 hover:text-pink-200 transition-colors" onClick={() => p().toggleMute()}>
                {muted || volume === 0 ? <VolumeX size={18} /> : <Volume2 size={18} />}
              </button>
              <input
                type="range"
                className="h-1.5 w-24 cursor-pointer accent-pink-400 rounded-lg bg-pink-950/60"
                min={0}
                max={1}
                step={0.01}
                value={muted ? 0 : volume}
                onChange={(e) => p().setVolume(Number(e.target.value))}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm font-medium text-pink-300/50">Выберите трек для воспроизведения</div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Рендерим полноэкранный плеер */}
      <FullScreenPlayer open={fullScreenOpen} onClose={() => setFullScreenOpen(false)} />
    </>
  );
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/PlaylistPicker.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Check, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { PlaylistSummary } from '@/lib/types';

/** Небольшое всплывающее окно «Добавить в плейлист». */
export function PlaylistPicker({ trackId, onClose }: { trackId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [lists, setLists] = useState<PlaylistSummary[] | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [done, setDone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api<{ items: PlaylistSummary[] }>('/playlists')
      .then((r) => setLists(r.items))
      .catch(() => setLists([]));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', onDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onDown);
  }, [onClose]);

  const add = async (playlistId: string, label: string) => {
    try {
      await api(`/playlists/${playlistId}/tracks`, { method: 'POST', body: { trackId } });
      setDone(label);
      setTimeout(onClose, 700);
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };

  const create = async () => {
    const n = name.trim();
    if (!n) return;
    try {
      const p = await api<PlaylistSummary>('/playlists', { method: 'POST', body: { name: n } });
      await add(p.id, p.name);
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="card absolute right-0 top-full z-30 mt-1 w-64 p-2 text-sm shadow-2xl" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
      {done ? (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-3 text-accent">
          <Check size={16} /> Добавлено в «{done}»
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
            {lists === null && <div className="px-2 py-2 text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>}
            {lists?.length === 0 && <div className="px-2 py-2 text-zinc-500">Плейлистов пока нет</div>}
            {lists?.map((p) => (
              <button
                key={p.id}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded px-2 py-1.5 text-left hover:bg-zinc-800"
                onClick={() => add(p.id, p.name)}
              >
                <span className="truncate">{p.name}</span>
                <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-zinc-500">{p.trackCount}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 flex gap-1 border-t border-zinc-800 pt-2">
            <input
              className="input !py-1"
              placeholder="Новый плейлист"
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && create()}
            />
            <button className="btn btn-primary !px-2" onClick={create} aria-label="Создать">
              <Plus size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
          {error && <div className="mt-1 px-1 text-xs text-red-400">{error}</div>}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/LoralLogo.tsx ---

export function LoralLogo({
  size = 28,
  withWordmark = true,
  className = '',
}: {
  size?: number;
  withWordmark?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className={`flex items-center gap-2 ${className}`}>
      <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 32 32" aria-hidden className="shrink-0">
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="loral-g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#f5c518" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#b8860b" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>
        <rect width="32" height="32" rx="8" fill="#111108" stroke="url(#loral-g)" strokeWidth="1.5" />
        <path d="M9 7.5h3.2v13.2H22V24H9V7.5Z" fill="url(#loral-g)" />
        <rect x="23.2" y="14.2" width="1.5" height="5.2" rx="0.6" fill="#f5c518" opacity="0.9" />
        <rect x="26" y="12.4" width="1.5" height="7" rx="0.6" fill="#f5c518" opacity="0.55" />
      </svg>
      {withWordmark && (
        <span className="text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-white">
          Lor<span className="text-accent">al</span>
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/SidebarMiniPlayer.tsx ---

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Compass, Heart, Import, Library, ListMusic, LogOut, Shield, Upload, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { Logo } from './Logo';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';
import { usePlayer } from '@/store/player';

interface NavItem {
  href: string;
  label: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  badge?: number;
}

export function Sidebar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const [badge, setBadge] = useState(0);

  const isAdmin = user?.role === 'ADMIN';
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    let alive = true;
    const load = () =>
      api<{ needsReview: number }>('/admin/summary')
        .then((s) => alive && setBadge(s.needsReview))
        .catch(() => undefined);
    void load();
    const t = setInterval(load, 60_000);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      clearInterval(t);
    };
  }, [isAdmin, pathname]);

  const items: NavItem[] = [
    { href: '/library', label: 'Библиотека', icon: Library },
    { href: '/browse', label: 'Обзор', icon: Compass },
    { href: '/favorites', label: 'Избранное', icon: Heart },
    { href: '/playlists', label: 'Плейлисты', icon: ListMusic },
    { href: '/import', label: 'Импорт', icon: Import },
    ...(user?.canUpload ? [{ href: '/upload', label: 'Загрузка', icon: Upload }] : []),
    ...(isAdmin ? [{ href: '/admin', label: 'Админ-панель', icon: Shield, badge }] : []),
  ];

  const active = (href: string) => pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);

  const logout = async () => {
    const { refreshToken, logout: clear } = useAuth.getState();
    if (refreshToken) await api('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', body: { refreshToken }, anonymous: true }).catch(() => undefined);
    usePlayer.getState().clear();
    clear();
    router.replace('/login');
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Десктопный Sidebar */}
      <aside className="hidden h-full min-h-0 w-64 shrink-0 flex-col border-r border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/20 backdrop-blur-xl p-4 md:flex">
        <Logo className="mb-6 px-2" />
        <nav className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {items.map(({ href, label, icon: Icon, badge: b }) => (
            <Link
              key={href}
              href={href}
              className={cn(
                'flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200',
                active(href)
                  ? 'bg-pink-500/20 border border-pink-400/30 text-pink-100 shadow-sm shadow-pink-950/40'
                  : 'text-pink-200/70 hover:bg-pink-900/20 hover:text-pink-100',
              )}
            >
              <Icon size={18} className={active(href) ? 'text-pink-300' : 'text-pink-300/60'} />
              <span className="flex-1">{label}</span>
              {!!b && (
                <span className="rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-400 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-pink-950 shadow-sm">
                  {b}
                </span>
              )}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="border-t border-pink-500/10 pt-3 mt-auto">
          <div className="mb-2 truncate px-2 text-xs text-pink-300/50">
            {user?.username} · {user?.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'админ' : 'пользователь'}
          </div>
          <button
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/30 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-pink-200/80 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-500/20 hover:border-rose-500/30 hover:text-rose-200"
            onClick={logout}
          >
            <LogOut size={16} /> Выйти
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* Мобильная навигация */}
      <nav className="flex shrink-0 gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto border-b border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/40 px-3 py-2.5 backdrop-blur-xl md:hidden">
        {items.map(({ href, label, icon: Icon, badge: b }) => (
          <Link
            key={href}
            href={href}
            className={cn(
              'flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-all',
              active(href)
                ? 'bg-pink-500/25 border border-pink-400/30 text-pink-100'
                : 'text-pink-200/70 bg-pink-950/30 border border-pink-500/10',
            )}
          >
            <Icon size={15} className={active(href) ? 'text-pink-300' : 'text-pink-300/60'} /> {label}
            {!!b && (
              <span className="rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-400 px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[10px] font-bold text-pink-950">
                {b}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>
        ))}
        <button
          className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-pink-500/10 bg-pink-950/30 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-pink-300/70"
          onClick={logout}
        >
          <LogOut size={15} /> Выйти
        </button>
      </nav>
    </>
  );
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/FullScreenPlayer.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Heart,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Repeat,
  Shuffle,
  SkipBack,
  SkipForward,
  X,
  ListMusic,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Cover } from '@/components/Cover';
import { usePlayer } from '@/store/player';
import { cn, formatDuration } from '@/lib/utils';

interface FullScreenPlayerProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function FullScreenPlayer({ open, onClose }: FullScreenPlayerProps) {
  const queue = usePlayer((s) => s.queue);
  const index = usePlayer((s) => s.index);
  const playing = usePlayer((s) => s.playing);
  const toggle = usePlayer((s) => s.toggle);
  const next = usePlayer((s) => s.next);
  const prev = usePlayer((s) => s.prev);
  const playQueue = usePlayer((s) => s.playQueue);

  const current = queue[index];

  const [showQueueSidebar, setShowQueueSidebar] = useState(true);
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
  const [trackDuration, setTrackDuration] = useState(0);
  const [isRepeating, setIsRepeating] = useState(false);
  const [isShuffling, setIsShuffling] = useState(false);

  // Синхронизация времени воспроизведения из аудиоэлемента
  useEffect(() => {
    const audio = document.querySelector('audio');
    if (!audio) return;

    const updateTime = () => setCurrentTime(audio.currentTime);
    const updateDuration = () => setTrackDuration(audio.duration || 0);

    audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', updateTime);
    audio.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', updateDuration);

    setCurrentTime(audio.currentTime);
    setTrackDuration(audio.duration || 0);

    return () => {
      audio.removeEventListener('timeupdate', updateTime);
      audio.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', updateDuration);
    };
  }, [open, current]);

  if (!open || !current) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex bg-pink-950/95 backdrop-blur-2xl text-pink-100 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Размытый фон из обложки текущего трека */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden pointer-events-none opacity-20">
        <Cover url={current.coverUrl} title={current.title} size={1200} className="w-full h-full object-cover blur-3xl scale-125" />
      </div>

      {/* Основная часть плеера (слева/по центру) */}
      <div className="relative flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between p-8 z-10 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
        {/* Верхняя панель */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-pink-300/60 font-semibold">
            Сейчас играет
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowQueueSidebar(!showQueueSidebar)}
              className={cn(
                'flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer',
                showQueueSidebar ? 'border-pink-400 bg-pink-500/20 text-pink-200' : 'border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-900/30 text-pink-300'
              )}
            >
              <ListMusic size={16} /> Очередь
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-900/30 p-2 text-pink-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-pink-800/40 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Центр: Большая обложка и информация о треке */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center my-auto py-4 space-y-6">
          <div className="relative group shadow-2xl rounded-3xl overflow-hidden border border-pink-500/20 w-72 h-72 sm:w-96 sm:h-96">
            <Cover url={current.coverUrl} title={current.title} size={500} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
          </div>

          <div className="text-center space-y-1 max-w-md">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold truncate text-pink-100">{current.title}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-pink-300/70 truncate">{current.artist}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Нижняя панель управления */}
        <div className="space-y-4 max-w-2xl mx-auto w-full pb-4">
          {/* Ползунок прогресса */}
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div
              className="relative h-2 w-full cursor-pointer rounded-full bg-pink-950/60 border border-pink-500/20 overflow-hidden group"
              onClick={(e) => {
                const audio = document.querySelector('audio');
                if (!audio || !trackDuration) return;
                const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
                const pos = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;
                audio.currentTime = pos * trackDuration;
              }}
            >
              <div
                className="absolute left-0 top-0 h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 via-rose-400 to-purple-400 rounded-full"
                style={{ width: `${(currentTime / (trackDuration || 1)) * 100}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-pink-300/60 tabular-nums">
              <span>{formatDuration(currentTime)}</span>
              <span>{formatDuration(trackDuration)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Кнопки плеера */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-6">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsShuffling(!isShuffling)}
              className={cn(
                'p-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer',
                isShuffling ? 'text-pink-400' : 'text-pink-300/40 hover:text-pink-200'
              )}
            >
              <Shuffle size={18} />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => prev()}
              className="p-2 text-pink-200 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <SkipBack size={24} fill="currentColor" />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => toggle()}
              className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 via-rose-400 to-purple-400 text-pink-950 shadow-lg hover:scale-105 transition-transform cursor-pointer"
            >
              {playing ? <Pause size={24} fill="currentColor" /> : <Play size={24} fill="currentColor" className="ml-0.5" />}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => next()}
              className="p-2 text-pink-200 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <SkipForward size={24} fill="currentColor" />
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsRepeating(!isRepeating)}
              className={cn(
                'p-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer',
                isRepeating ? 'text-pink-400' : 'text-pink-300/40 hover:text-pink-200'
              )}
            >
              <Repeat size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Правая панель: Очередь воспроизведения */}
      {showQueueSidebar && (
        <div className="w-80 border-l border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/80 backdrop-blur-xl flex flex-col z-20 shadow-2xl">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-pink-500/15 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-pink-100">Очередь воспроизведения</h3>
            <span className="text-xs text-pink-300/50">{queue.length} треков</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-2 space-y-1 divide-y divide-pink-500/10">
            {queue.map((track, i) => {
              const isCurrent = i === index;
              return (
                <div
                  key={`${track.id}-${i}`}
                  onClick={() => playQueue(queue, i)}
                  className={cn(
                    'flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 rounded-xl cursor-pointer transition-colors group',
                    isCurrent ? 'bg-pink-500/20 border border-pink-500/30' : 'hover:bg-pink-900/30'
                  )}
                >
                  <Cover url={track.coverUrl} title={track.title} size={36} className="rounded-lg shrink-0" />
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className={cn('truncate text-xs font-semibold', isCurrent ? 'text-pink-300' : 'text-pink-100')}>
                      {track.title}
                    </div>
                    <div className="truncate text-[11px] text-pink-300/60">{track.artist}</div>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[11px] tabular-nums text-pink-300/40">{formatDuration(track.duration)}</span>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/TrackTableWithSelection.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { CheckSquare, Square, Trash2, Heart, PlusCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import type { Track } from '@/lib/types';

interface Props {
  tracks: Track[];
  onDeleteSelected?: (ids: string[]) => void;
  onAddToPlaylistSelected?: (ids: string[]) => void;
  onFavoriteSelected?: (ids: string[]) => void;
  onPlayTrack?: (track: Track, index: number) => void;
}

export function TrackTableWithSelection({
  tracks,
  onDeleteSelected,
  onAddToPlaylistSelected,
  onFavoriteSelected,
  onPlayTrack,
}: Props) {
  const [selectMode, setSelectMode] = useState(false);
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

  const toggleSelectAll = () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === tracks.length) {
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
    } else {
      setSelectedIds(new Set(tracks.map((t) => t.id)));
    }
  };

  const toggleTrack = (id: string) => {
    const next = new Set(selectedIds);
    if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
    else next.add(id);
    setSelectedIds(next);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {/* Кнопка активации режима выбора */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-2 py-1">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setSelectMode(!selectMode);
            setSelectedIds(new Set());
          }}
          className={cn(
            'flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold transition-all border',
            selectMode
              ? 'bg-pink-500/25 border-pink-400/50 text-pink-100 shadow-sm'
              : 'bg-pink-950/40 border-pink-500/20 text-pink-300 hover:bg-pink-900/30',
          )}
        >
          <CheckSquare size={16} />
          {selectMode ? 'Выйти из режима выбора' : 'Выбрать несколько треков'}
        </button>

        {selectMode && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleSelectAll}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-300 hover:text-pink-100 transition-colors"
          >
            {selectedIds.size === tracks.length ? <CheckSquare size={16} /> : <Square size={16} />}
            {selectedIds.size === tracks.length ? 'Снять выделение' : 'Выбрать все'}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Панель массовых действий */}
      {selectMode && selectedIds.size > 0 && (
        <div className="sticky top-4 z-20 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/30 bg-pink-950/90 p-3.5 shadow-xl backdrop-blur-xl">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-pink-200 pl-2">
            Выбрано: {selectedIds.size}
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {onFavoriteSelected && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onFavoriteSelected(Array.from(selectedIds))}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-pink-900/50 border border-pink-500/30 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-200 hover:bg-pink-800/60"
              >
                <Heart size={14} className="text-pink-400" />
                В любимые
              </button>
            )}
            {onAddToPlaylistSelected && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onAddToPlaylistSelected(Array.from(selectedIds))}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-pink-900/50 border border-pink-500/30 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-200 hover:bg-pink-800/60"
              >
                <PlusCircle size={14} />
                В плейлист
              </button>
            )}
            {onDeleteSelected && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onDeleteSelected(Array.from(selectedIds))}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/20 border border-rose-500/40 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-200 hover:bg-rose-500/30"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} />
                Удалить
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/Cover.tsx ---

import { assetUrl } from '@/lib/api';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export function Cover({
  url,
  title,
  size = 40,
  className,
}: {
  url?: string | null;
  title: string;
  size?: number;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const src = assetUrl(url);
  if (src) {
    return (
      // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
      <img
        src={src}
        alt=""
        width={size}
        height={size}
        loading="lazy"
        className={cn('shrink-0 rounded bg-zinc-800 object-cover', className)}
        style={{ width: size, height: size }}
      />
    );
  }
  const hue = 28 + ([...title].reduce((acc, ch) => acc + ch.charCodeAt(0), 0) % 28); // янтарная гамма
  return (
    <div
      className={cn('flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded font-semibold text-white/70', className)}
      style={{
        width: size,
        height: size,
        fontSize: Math.max(size * 0.4, 10),
        background: `linear-gradient(135deg, hsl(${hue} 70% 38%), hsl(${hue - 10} 60% 14%))`,
      }}
    >
      {title.trim()[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? '♪'}
    </div>
  );
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/Sidebar.tsx ---

'use client';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Compass, Library, LogOut, Shield, Heart, ListMusic, Import, Upload } from 'lucide-react';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Logo } from './Logo';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export function Sidebar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const [badge, setBadge] = useState<number>(0);

  const isAdmin = user?.role === 'ADMIN';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    let mounted = true;
    const fetchStats = () => {
      api<any>('/admin/summary').then((s) => mounted && setBadge(s.needsReview)).catch(() => {});
    };
    fetchStats();
    const int = setInterval(fetchStats, 60000);
    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      clearInterval(int);
    };
  }, [isAdmin]);

  const links = [
    { href: '/library', label: 'Библиотека', icon: Library },
    { href: '/browse', label: 'Обзор', icon: Compass },
    { href: '/favorites', label: 'Избранное', icon: Heart },
    { href: '/playlists', label: 'Плейлисты', icon: ListMusic },
    { href: '/import', label: 'Импорт', icon: Import },
    ...(user?.canUpload ? [{ href: '/upload', label: 'Загрузка', icon: Upload }] : []),
    ...(isAdmin ? [{ href: '/admin', label: 'Админ-панель', icon: Shield, badge }] : []),
  ];

  const isActive = (href: string) => pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    const { refreshToken, logout } = useAuth.getState();
    if (refreshToken) {
      await api('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST', body: { refreshToken }, anonymous: true }).catch(() => {});
    }
    
    localStorage.clear();
    sessionStorage.clear();
    document.cookie.split(";").forEach((c) => {
      document.cookie = c.replace(/^ +/, "").replace(/=.*/, "=;expires=" + new Date().toUTCString() + ";path=/");
    });
    
    logout();
    router.replace('/login');
    setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 100);
  };

  if (!user) return null;

  return (
    <>
      {/* ДОБАВЛЕН КЛАСС pb-[104px] ЧТОБЫ КНОПКА НЕ ПРЯТАЛАСЬ ПОД ПЛЕЕРОМ */}
      <aside className="hidden h-full min-h-0 w-64 shrink-0 flex-col border-r border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/20 backdrop-blur-xl p-4 pb-[104px] md:flex">
        <Logo className="mb-6 px-2" />
        <nav className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {links.map(({ href, label, icon: Icon, badge: num }) => (
            <Link
              key={href}
              href={href}
              className={cn(
                'flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200',
                isActive(href)
                  ? 'bg-pink-500/20 border border-pink-400/30 text-pink-100 shadow-sm shadow-pink-950/40'
                  : 'text-pink-200/70 hover:bg-pink-900/20 hover:text-pink-100'
              )}
            >
              <Icon size={18} className={isActive(href) ? 'text-pink-300' : 'text-pink-300/60'} />
              <span className="flex-1">{label}</span>
              {!!num && (
                <span className="rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-400 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-pink-950 shadow-sm">
                  {num}
                </span>
              )}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="border-t border-pink-500/10 pt-3 mt-auto">
          <div className="mb-2 truncate px-2 text-xs text-pink-300/50">
            {user?.username} &middot; {user?.role === 'ADMIN' ? 'админ' : 'пользователь'}
          </div>
          <button
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/30 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-pink-200/80 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-500/20 hover:border-rose-500/30 hover:text-rose-200 cursor-pointer"
            onClick={handleLogout}
          >
            <LogOut size={16} /> Выйти
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <nav className="flex shrink-0 gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto border-b border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/40 px-3 py-2.5 backdrop-blur-xl md:hidden">
        {links.map(({ href, label, icon: Icon, badge: num }) => (
          <Link
            key={href}
            href={href}
            className={cn(
              'flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-all',
              isActive(href)
                ? 'bg-pink-500/25 border border-pink-400/30 text-pink-100'
                : 'text-pink-200/70 bg-pink-950/30 border border-pink-500/10'
            )}
          >
            <Icon size={15} className={isActive(href) ? 'text-pink-300' : 'text-pink-300/60'} /> {label}
            {!!num && (
              <span className="rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-400 px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[10px] font-bold text-pink-950">
                {num}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>
        ))}
        <button
          className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-pink-500/10 bg-pink-950/30 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-pink-300/70 cursor-pointer"
          onClick={handleLogout}
        >
          <LogOut size={15} /> Выйти
        </button>
      </nav>
    </>
  );
}

'vener client';

import Link from 'next/link';

--- ФАЙЛ: ./components/TrackTable.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Heart, ListEnd, ListPlus, Pause, Play, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { Track } from '@/lib/types';
import { cn, formatDuration } from '@/lib/utils';
import { trackToItem, usePlayer } from '@/store/player';
import { Cover } from './Cover';
import { PlaylistPicker } from './PlaylistPicker';

interface Props {
  tracks: Track[];
  onPlay: (index: number) => void;
  onFavoriteChange?: (trackId: string, favorite: boolean) => void;
  /** если передан — у каждой строки появляется кнопка удаления трека из медиатеки */
  onDelete?: (trackId: string) => void;
  /** дополнительные кнопки в конце строки (например, «убрать из плейлиста») */
  renderExtra?: (track: Track, index: number) => ReactNode;
  emptyText?: string;
}

export function TrackTable({ tracks, onPlay, onFavoriteChange, onDelete, renderExtra, emptyText = 'Ничего не найдено' }: Props) {
  const current = usePlayer((s) => s.queue[s.index]);
  const playing = usePlayer((s) => s.playing);
  const [favs, setFavs] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [pickerFor, setPickerFor] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const isFav = (t: Track) => favs[t.id] ?? t.isFavorite ?? false;

  const toggleFav = async (t: Track) => {
    const next = !isFav(t);
    setFavs((f) => ({ ...f, [t.id]: next }));
    try {
      await api(`/favorites/${t.id}`, { method: next ? 'PUT' : 'DELETE' });
      onFavoriteChange?.(t.id, next);
    } catch {
      setFavs((f) => ({ ...f, [t.id]: !next }));
    }
  };

  const remove = async (t: Track) => {
    if (!confirm(`Удалить «${t.artist} — ${t.title}» из медиатеки? Файл будет удалён с сервера.`)) return;
    try {
      await api(`/tracks/${t.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    } catch (e) {
      alert((e as Error).message);
      return;
    }
    // убираем удалённый трек из очереди плеера, иначе он попытается его воспроизвести
    const p = usePlayer.getState();
    p.queue
      .map((q, i) => (q.kind === 'local' && q.id === t.id ? i : -1))
      .filter((i) => i >= 0)
      .reverse()
      .forEach((i) => usePlayer.getState().removeAt(i));
    onDelete?.(t.id);
  };

  if (tracks.length === 0) return <div className="card px-6 py-12 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">{emptyText}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="card divide-y divide-zinc-900">
      <div className="hidden grid-cols-[2.5rem_minmax(0,2fr)_minmax(0,1.3fr)_4rem_4rem_8.5rem] gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500 md:grid">
        <span>#</span>
        <span>Название</span>
        <span>Альбом · жанры</span>
        <span>Год</span>
        <span>Время</span>
        <span />
      </div>
      {tracks.map((t, i) => {
        const isCurrent = current?.kind === 'local' && current.id === t.id;
        return (
          <div
            key={t.id}
            className={cn(
              'group relative grid grid-cols-[2.5rem_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 hover:bg-zinc-900/60 md:grid-cols-[2.5rem_minmax(0,2fr)_minmax(0,1.3fr)_4rem_4rem_8.5rem]',
              isCurrent && 'bg-zinc-900/80',
            )}
            onDoubleClick={() => onPlay(i)}
          >
            <button
              className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded text-zinc-500 hover:text-white"
              onClick={() => (isCurrent ? usePlayer.getState().toggle() : onPlay(i))}
              aria-label={isCurrent && playing ? 'Пауза' : 'Играть'}
            >
              {isCurrent && playing ? (
                <Pause size={16} className="text-accent" fill="currentColor" />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <span className={cn('text-xs tabular-nums group-hover:hidden', isCurrent && 'text-accent')}>{i + 1}</span>
                  <Play size={16} className="hidden group-hover:block" fill="currentColor" />
                </>
              )}
            </button>

            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
              <Cover url={t.coverUrl} title={t.title} size={40} />
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className={cn('truncate text-sm', isCurrent && 'text-accent')}>{t.title}</div>
                <div className="truncate text-xs text-zinc-400">{t.artist}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="hidden min-w-0 md:block">
              <div className="truncate text-xs text-zinc-400">{t.album ?? '—'}</div>
              <div className="mt-0.5 flex gap-1 overflow-hidden">
                {t.genres.slice(0, 3).map((g) => (
                  <span
                    key={g.name}
                    className={cn(
                      'truncate rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px]',
                      g.primary ? 'bg-yellow-400/15 text-yellow-300' : 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-400',
                    )}
                  >
                    {g.name}
                  </span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="hidden text-xs text-zinc-400 md:block">{t.year ?? '—'}</div>
            <div className="hidden text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-400 md:block">{formatDuration(t.duration)}</div>

            <div className="relative flex items-center justify-end gap-0.5">
              <button
                className={cn('rounded p-1.5', isFav(t) ? 'text-accent' : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-white')}
                onClick={() => toggleFav(t)}
                aria-label="В избранное"
                aria-pressed={isFav(t)}
              >
                <Heart size={16} fill={isFav(t) ? 'currentColor' : 'none'} />
              </button>
              <button
                className="rounded p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-white"
                onClick={() => usePlayer.getState().enqueue(trackToItem(t))}
                title="В очередь"
              >
                <ListEnd size={16} />
              </button>
              <button
                className="rounded p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-white"
                onClick={() => setPickerFor(pickerFor === t.id ? null : t.id)}
                title="В плейлист"
              >
                <ListPlus size={16} />
              </button>
              {onDelete && (
                <button className="rounded p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-red-400" onClick={() => void remove(t)} title="Удалить из медиатеки">
                  <Trash2 size={16} />
                </button>
              )}
              {renderExtra?.(t, i)}
              {pickerFor === t.id && <PlaylistPicker trackId={t.id} onClose={() => setPickerFor(null)} />}
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./lib/filename.ts ---

// Клиентская копия правила именования (сервер всё равно проверяет повторно).

export const AUDIO_EXTENSIONS = ['.mp3', '.flac', '.wav', '.ogg', '.m4a', '.opus'];
export const NAMING_HINT = 'Переименуйте файл в формат «Исполнитель - Название.mp3» (дефис с пробелами по бокам).';

export function validateUploadName(fileName: string): { ok: true; artist: string; title: string } | { ok: false; error: string } {
  const dot = fileName.lastIndexOf('.');
  const ext = dot > 0 ? fileName.slice(dot).toLowerCase() : '';
  if (!AUDIO_EXTENSIONS.includes(ext)) {
    return { ok: false, error: `Неподдерживаемый формат. Допустимо: ${AUDIO_EXTENSIONS.join(', ')}` };
  }
  const base = fileName.slice(0, dot).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const idx = base.indexOf(' - ');
  const artist = idx > 0 ? base.slice(0, idx).trim() : '';
  const title = idx > 0 ? base.slice(idx + 3).trim() : '';
  if (!artist || !title) return { ok: false, error: `Имя файла не соответствует правилу. ${NAMING_HINT}` };
  return { ok: true, artist, title };
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./lib/utils.ts ---

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function formatDuration(sec: number | undefined | null): string {
  if (!sec || !Number.isFinite(sec) || sec < 0) return '0:00';
  const s = Math.floor(sec);
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
  const r = s % 60;
  return h > 0 ? `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}` : `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (!bytes) return '0 Б';
  const units = ['Б', 'КБ', 'МБ', 'ГБ', 'ТБ'];
  const i = Math.min(Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(1024)), units.length - 1);
  return `${(bytes / 1024 ** i).toFixed(i === 0 ? 0 : 1)} ${units[i]}`;
}

export function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('ru-RU', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
}

export function cn(...parts: (string | false | null | undefined)[]): string {
  return parts.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}

export function useDebounced<T>(value: T, delay = 300): T {
  const [v, setV] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setV(value), delay);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [value, delay]);
  return v;
}

/** «2024-05-31» → начало/конец дня в локальной зоне, в ISO для API */
export function dayStartISO(date: string): string {
  return new Date(`${date}T00:00:00`).toISOString();
}
export function dayEndISO(date: string): string {
  return new Date(`${date}T23:59:59.999`).toISOString();
}

export function youtubeSearchUrl(artist: string, title: string): string {
  return `https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=${encodeURIComponent(`${artist} ${title}`)}`;
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./lib/api.ts ---

import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';
import type { Session } from '@/lib/types';

export const API_BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? '/api';

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public status: number,
    public code: string,
    message: string,
    public data?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

type Query = Record<string, string | number | boolean | null | undefined>;

function buildUrl(path: string, query?: Query): string {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(query ?? {})) {
    if (v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== '') qs.set(k, String(v));
  }
  const s = qs.toString();
  return `${API_BASE}${path}${s ? `?${s}` : ''}`;
}

/** Обложки отдаются бэкендом как /api/covers/…; при прямом API_URL подставляем его origin. */
export function assetUrl(url: string | null | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (!url) return undefined;
  if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) return url;
  if (API_BASE.startsWith('http')) return API_BASE.replace(/\/api\/?$/, '') + url;
  return url;
}

export function streamUrl(trackId: string): string {
  const token = useAuth.getState().streamToken ?? '';
  return `${API_BASE}/tracks/${trackId}/stream?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`;
}

let refreshing: Promise<boolean> | null = null;

/** Обновляет пару токенов по refresh-токену (один запрос на всех параллельных вызовов). */
export function refreshSession(): Promise<boolean> {
  refreshing ??= (async () => {
    const { refreshToken, setSession, logout } = useAuth.getState();
    if (!refreshToken) return false;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/refresh`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ refreshToken }),
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) logout();
        return false;
      }
      setSession((await res.json()) as Session);
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  })().finally(() => {
    refreshing = null;
  });
  return refreshing;
}

export async function refreshStreamToken(): Promise<void> {
  const r = await api<{ streamToken: string }>('/auth/stream-token');
  useAuth.getState().setStreamToken(r.streamToken);
}

interface ApiOptions {
  method?: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'PATCH' | 'DELETE';
  body?: unknown;
  query?: Query;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  /** не пытаться обновлять сессию (для /auth/login и т.п.) */
  anonymous?: boolean;
}

export async function api<T = unknown>(path: string, opts: ApiOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
  const doFetch = () => {
    const token = useAuth.getState().accessToken;
    return fetch(buildUrl(path, opts.query), {
      method: opts.method ?? 'GET',
      headers: {
        ...(opts.body !== undefined ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
        ...(token && !opts.anonymous ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
      },
      body: opts.body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(opts.body) : undefined,
      signal: opts.signal,
    });
  };

  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await doFetch();
  } catch (e) {
    if ((e as Error).name === 'AbortError') throw e;
    throw new ApiError(0, 'network', 'Нет связи с сервером');
  }

  if (res.status === 401 && !opts.anonymous && useAuth.getState().refreshToken) {
    if (await refreshSession()) res = await doFetch();
  }
  if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;

  const data = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as (Record<string, unknown> & { error?: string; message?: string }) | null;
  if (!res.ok) {
    if (res.status === 401 && !opts.anonymous) useAuth.getState().logout();
    throw new ApiError(res.status, data?.error ?? 'error', data?.message ?? res.statusText, data ?? undefined);
  }
  return data as T;
}

export interface UploadOutcome {
  fileName: string;
  status: 'created' | 'duplicate' | 'error';
  error?: string;
}

/** 
 * Загрузка одного файла в личную медиатеку с прогрессом и повторами при ошибках 5xx / сбоях сети.
 */
export function uploadFile(file: File, onProgress: (pct: number) => void): Promise<UploadOutcome> {
  const maxRetries = 3;

  const sendOnce = (canRetrySession: boolean): Promise<UploadOutcome> => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
      xhr.open('POST', `${API_BASE}/upload`);
      xhr.setRequestHeader('Authorization', `Bearer ${useAuth.getState().accessToken ?? ''}`);
      
      xhr.upload.onprogress = (e) => {
        if (e.lengthComputable) onProgress(Math.round((e.loaded / e.total) * 100));
      };

      xhr.onerror = () => reject(new ApiError(0, 'network', 'Нет связи с сервером'));
      
      xhr.onload = async () => {
        if (xhr.status === 401 && canRetrySession && (await refreshSession())) {
          return sendOnce(false).then(resolve, reject);
        }
        let data: { results?: UploadOutcome[]; message?: string; error?: string } = {};
        try {
          data = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
        } catch {
          /* пустой ответ */
        }
        if (xhr.status >= 400) {
          return reject(new ApiError(xhr.status, data.error ?? 'error', data.message ?? `Ошибка ${xhr.status}`));
        }
        resolve(data.results?.[0] ?? { fileName: file.name, status: 'error', error: 'Пустой ответ сервера' });
      };

      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append('files', file, file.name);
      xhr.send(fd);
    });
  };

  return (async () => {
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
      try {
        return await sendOnce(true);
      } catch (err) {
        if (err instanceof ApiError) {
          // Если ошибка на стороне клиента (4xx, кроме 429), не повторяем
          if (err.status >= 400 && err.status < 500 && err.status !== 429) {
            throw err;
          }
        }
        if (attempt === maxRetries - 1) throw err;
        // Пауза 2 секунды перед повтором при 500 / 502 / network error
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000));
      }
    }
    throw new ApiError(0, 'network', 'Не удалось загрузить файл после нескольких попыток');
  })();
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./store/player.ts ---

'use client';

import { create } from 'zustand';
import { createJSONStorage, persist } from 'zustand/middleware';
import type { Track } from '@/lib/types';

export interface QueueItem {
  key: string;
  kind: 'local' | 'preview';
  /** id трека в нашей библиотеке (для kind = local) */
  id?: string;
  title: string;
  artist: string;
  coverUrl?: string | null;
  duration?: number;
  /** прямой URL (для превью) */
  src?: string;
}

export type NewQueueItem = Omit<QueueItem, 'key'>;
export type RepeatMode = 'off' | 'all' | 'one';

/** <audio> живёт в GlobalPlayer, а стор управляет им через эту ссылку. */
export const audioRef: { current: HTMLAudioElement | null } = { current: null };

let seq = 0;
const nextKey = () => `q${++seq}`;
const withKey = (i: NewQueueItem): QueueItem => ({ ...i, key: nextKey() });

export function trackToItem(t: Track): NewQueueItem {
  return { kind: 'local', id: t.id, title: t.title, artist: t.artist, coverUrl: t.coverUrl, duration: t.duration };
}

interface PlayerState {
  queue: QueueItem[];
  index: number;
  playing: boolean;
  shuffle: boolean;
  repeat: RepeatMode;
  volume: number;
  muted: boolean;
  currentTime: number;
  duration: number;
  /** индексы уже сыгранных треков (для shuffle и кнопки «назад») */
  history: number[];
  queueOpen: boolean;
  error: string | null;

  playQueue: (items: NewQueueItem[], start?: number) => void;
  toggle: () => void;
  next: (auto?: boolean) => void;
  prev: () => void;
  seek: (t: number) => void;
  setVolume: (v: number) => void;
  toggleMute: () => void;
  toggleShuffle: () => void;
  cycleRepeat: () => void;
  enqueue: (item: NewQueueItem) => void;
  playNext: (item: NewQueueItem) => void;
  removeAt: (i: number) => void;
  jumpTo: (i: number) => void;
  clear: () => void;
  setQueueOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  // служебные — вызываются из GlobalPlayer
  _tick: (t: number, d: number) => void;
  _setPlaying: (p: boolean) => void;
  _setError: (e: string | null) => void;
}

const range = (n: number) => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i);

function restartCurrent() {
  const a = audioRef.current;
  if (a) {
    a.currentTime = 0;
    void a.play().catch(() => undefined);
  }
}

export const usePlayer = create<PlayerState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => {
      const pushHistory = (s: PlayerState, idx: number) => (s.shuffle ? [...s.history, idx] : [idx]);

      return {
        queue: [],
        index: 0,
        playing: false,
        shuffle: false,
        repeat: 'off',
        volume: 0.8,
        muted: false,
        currentTime: 0,
        duration: 0,
        history: [],
        queueOpen: false,
        error: null,

        playQueue: (items, start = 0) => {
          if (items.length === 0) return;
          const idx = Math.min(Math.max(start, 0), items.length - 1);
          set({ queue: items.map(withKey), index: idx, history: [idx], playing: true, currentTime: 0, duration: 0, error: null });
        },

        toggle: () => {
          const s = get();
          if (s.queue.length === 0) return;
          set({ playing: !s.playing, error: null });
        },

        next: (auto = false) => {
          const s = get();
          const n = s.queue.length;
          if (n === 0) return;
          if (auto && s.repeat === 'one') return restartCurrent();

          let nextIdx: number | null = null;
          let history = s.history;
          if (s.shuffle && n > 1) {
            const played = new Set(history);
            let candidates = range(n).filter((i) => !played.has(i));
            if (candidates.length === 0 && s.repeat === 'all') {
              history = [s.index]; // новый круг
              candidates = range(n).filter((i) => i !== s.index);
            }
            if (candidates.length) nextIdx = candidates[Math.floor(Math.random() * candidates.length)]!;
          } else if (s.index + 1 < n) {
            nextIdx = s.index + 1;
          } else if (s.repeat === 'all') {
            nextIdx = 0;
          }

          if (nextIdx === null) {
            if (auto) set({ playing: false, currentTime: 0 }); // очередь закончилась
            return;
          }
          if (nextIdx === s.index) return restartCurrent(); // в очереди один трек
          set({ index: nextIdx, history: pushHistory({ ...s, history }, nextIdx), playing: true, currentTime: 0, error: null });
        },

        prev: () => {
          const s = get();
          const a = audioRef.current;
          if (a && a.currentTime > 3) {
            a.currentTime = 0;
            return;
          }
          if (s.shuffle) {
            if (s.history.length > 1) {
              const h = s.history.slice(0, -1);
              set({ index: h[h.length - 1]!, history: h, playing: true, currentTime: 0, error: null });
            } else if (a) a.currentTime = 0;
            return;
          }
          const target = s.index > 0 ? s.index - 1 : s.repeat === 'all' ? s.queue.length - 1 : -1;
          if (target < 0 || target === s.index) {
            if (a) a.currentTime = 0;
            return;
          }
          set({ index: target, history: [target], playing: true, currentTime: 0, error: null });
        },

        seek: (t) => {
          const a = audioRef.current;
          if (a && Number.isFinite(t)) {
            a.currentTime = t;
            set({ currentTime: t });
          }
        },

        setVolume: (v) => set({ volume: Math.min(Math.max(v, 0), 1), muted: false }),
        toggleMute: () => set((s) => ({ muted: !s.muted })),
        toggleShuffle: () => set((s) => ({ shuffle: !s.shuffle, history: [s.index] })),
        cycleRepeat: () => set((s) => ({ repeat: s.repeat === 'off' ? 'all' : s.repeat === 'all' ? 'one' : 'off' })),

        enqueue: (item) => {
          const s = get();
          if (s.queue.length === 0) return get().playQueue([item]);
          set({ queue: [...s.queue, withKey(item)] });
        },

        playNext: (item) => {
          const s = get();
          if (s.queue.length === 0) return get().playQueue([item]);
          const at = s.index + 1;
          const queue = [...s.queue.slice(0, at), withKey(item), ...s.queue.slice(at)];
          set({ queue, history: s.history.map((h) => (h >= at ? h + 1 : h)) });
        },

        removeAt: (i) => {
          const s = get();
          const queue = s.queue.filter((_, k) => k !== i);
          if (queue.length === 0) return get().clear();
          let index = s.index;
          if (i < index) index -= 1;
          else if (i === index) index = Math.min(index, queue.length - 1); // продолжит следующий трек
          const history = s.history.filter((h) => h !== i).map((h) => (h > i ? h - 1 : h));
          set({ queue, index, history: history.length ? history : [index] });
        },

        jumpTo: (i) => {
          const s = get();
          if (i < 0 || i >= s.queue.length) return;
          set({ index: i, history: pushHistory(s, i), playing: true, currentTime: 0, error: null });
        },

        clear: () => {
          audioRef.current?.pause();
          set({ queue: [], index: 0, history: [], playing: false, currentTime: 0, duration: 0, error: null });
        },

        setQueueOpen: (queueOpen) => set({ queueOpen }),

        _tick: (currentTime, duration) => set({ currentTime, duration: Number.isFinite(duration) ? duration : 0 }),
        _setPlaying: (playing) => set({ playing }),
        _setError: (error) => set({ error, ...(error ? { playing: false } : {}) }),
      };
    },
    {
      name: 'ms-player',
      storage: createJSONStorage(() => localStorage),
      partialize: (s) => ({ volume: s.volume, shuffle: s.shuffle, repeat: s.repeat }),
    },
  ),
);


--- ФАЙЛ: ./store/auth.ts ---

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { createJSONStorage, persist } from 'zustand/middleware';
import type { Session, User } from '@/lib/types';

interface AuthState {
  user: User | null;
  accessToken: string | null;
  refreshToken: string | null;
  streamToken: string | null;
  setSession: (s: Session) => void;
  setUser: (u: User) => void;
  setStreamToken: (t: string) => void;
  logout: () => void;
}

export const useAuth = create<AuthState>()(
  persist(
    (set) => ({
      user: null,
      accessToken: null,
      refreshToken: null,
      streamToken: null,
      setSession: (s) =>
        set({ user: s.user, accessToken: s.accessToken, refreshToken: s.refreshToken, streamToken: s.streamToken }),
      setUser: (user) => set({ user }),
      setStreamToken: (streamToken) => set({ streamToken }),
      logout: () => set({ user: null, accessToken: null, refreshToken: null, streamToken: null }),
    }),
    {
      name: 'ms-auth',
      storage: createJSONStorage(() => localStorage),
    },
  ),
);

/** true, когда состояние из localStorage уже подгружено (избегаем мигания экрана входа). */
export function useAuthHydrated(): boolean {
  const [hydrated, setHydrated] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (useAuth.persist.hasHydrated()) setHydrated(true);
    return useAuth.persist.onFinishHydration(() => setHydrated(true));
  }, []);
  return hydrated;
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/login/page.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Lock, Mail, User, Loader2, Music2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Logo } from '@/components/Logo';
import { api, ApiError } from '@/lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'login' | 'register'>('login');
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const endpoint = mode === 'login' ? '/auth/login' : '/auth/register';
      
      // Передаем и username, и login одновременно, чтобы бэкенд точно нашел нужное поле
      const body: any = {
        username,
        login: username,
        password,
      };

      if (mode === 'register' && email) {
        body.email = email;
      }

      const res = await api<{ accessToken: string; refreshToken: string; user: any }>(endpoint, {
        method: 'POST',
        body,
        anonymous: true,
      });

      const store = useAuth.getState();
      if (typeof (store as any).setAuth === 'function') {
        (store as any).setAuth(res.user, res.accessToken, res.refreshToken);
      } else if (typeof (store as any).login === 'function') {
        (store as any).login(res.user, res.accessToken, res.refreshToken);
      } else {
        useAuth.setState({
          user: res.user,
          accessToken: res.accessToken,
          refreshToken: res.refreshToken,
        });
      }

      router.replace('/library');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Произошла ошибка соединения');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full items-center justify-center p-4">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-5 rounded-3xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/40 p-8 shadow-2xl shadow-pink-950/80 backdrop-blur-xl">
        
        {/* Логотип */}
        <div className="flex justify-center pb-2">
          <Logo />
        </div>

        {/* Переключатель Вход / Регистрация */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/50 p-1 text-sm">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => { setMode('login'); setError(null); }}
            className={`rounded-xl py-2 font-medium transition-all ${
              mode === 'login'
                ? 'bg-pink-500/30 border border-pink-400/40 text-pink-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-pink-300/60 hover:text-pink-200'
            }`}
          >
            Вход
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => { setMode('register'); setError(null); }}
            className={`rounded-xl py-2 font-medium transition-all ${
              mode === 'register'
                ? 'bg-pink-500/30 border border-pink-400/40 text-pink-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-pink-300/60 hover:text-pink-200'
            }`}
          >
            Регистрация
          </button>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 p-3 text-center text-xs font-medium text-rose-200">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-pink-300/85">Имя пользователя / Логин</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <User size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-pink-400/50" />
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={username}
                onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Введите логин"
                className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/60 py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-pink-100 placeholder-pink-300/30 outline-none transition-all focus:border-pink-400 focus:bg-pink-900/40 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-pink-500/20"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Поле Email появляется при регистрации */}
          {mode === 'register' && (
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-pink-300/85">Email</label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-pink-400/50" />
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="name@example.com"
                  className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/60 py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-pink-100 placeholder-pink-300/30 outline-none transition-all focus:border-pink-400 focus:bg-pink-900/40 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-pink-500/20"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-pink-300/85">Пароль</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Lock size={16} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-pink-400/50" />
              <input
                type="password"
                required
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="w-full rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/60 py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm text-pink-100 placeholder-pink-300/30 outline-none transition-all focus:border-pink-400 focus:bg-pink-900/40 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-pink-500/20"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading}
          className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 via-rose-400 to-purple-400 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-pink-950 shadow-lg shadow-pink-500/25 transition-all hover:opacity-95 active:scale-[0.99] disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loading ? (
            <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" />
          ) : (
            <Music2 size={18} />
          )}
          {mode === 'login' ? 'Войти в Кими' : 'Создать аккаунт'}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/layout.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { GlobalPlayer } from '@/components/GlobalPlayer';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/Sidebar';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { User } from '@/lib/types';
import { useAuth, useAuthHydrated } from '@/store/auth';

/**
 * Этот layout не перемонтируется при навигации между страницами группы (app),
 * поэтому <GlobalPlayer/> с единственным <audio> живёт непрерывно — музыка не прерывается.
 */
export default function AppLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const hydrated = useAuthHydrated();
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hydrated && !user) {
      // Spotify вернул пользователя на /import до входа — запоминаем код авторизации
      if (location.pathname === '/import' && location.search.includes('code=')) {
        sessionStorage.setItem('loral-pending-import', location.search);
      }
      router.replace('/login');
    }
  }, [hydrated, user, router]);

  // Актуализируем роль/права (их мог изменить админ)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    api<{ user: User }>('/auth/me')
      .then((r) => useAuth.getState().setUser(r.user))
      .catch(() => undefined);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [user?.id]);

  if (!hydrated || !user) {
    return <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen flex-col md:flex-row">
      <Sidebar />
      <main className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto pb-[104px]">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl p-4 md:p-8">{children}</div>
      </main>
      <GlobalPlayer />
    </div>
  );
}
