--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/browse/page.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, Check, Download, ExternalLink, Pause, Play, Youtube } from 'lucide-react';
import { Cover } from '@/components/Cover';
import { api, ApiError } from '@/lib/api';
import type { DiscoverMeta, DiscoverResponse, ExternalItem } from '@/lib/types';
import { cn, formatDuration, youtubeSearchUrl } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';
import { usePlayer, type NewQueueItem } from '@/store/player';

type Tab = 'top' | 'genre';

const TABS: { id: Tab; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'top', label: 'Топ-50 недели' },
  { id: 'genre', label: 'По жанрам' },
];

const DEFAULT_GENRES = [
  { tag: 'pop', label: 'Поп' },
  { tag: 'hip-hop', label: 'Хип-хоп / Рэп' },
  { tag: 'rock', label: 'Рок' },
  { tag: 'electronic', label: 'Электроника' },
  { tag: 'rnb', label: 'R&B' },
  { tag: 'indie', label: 'Инди' },
  { tag: 'dance', label: 'Танцевальная' },
  { tag: 'metal', label: 'Метал' },
];

const TONES: Record<string, string> = {
  'hip-hop': 'from-pink-600 to-rose-950',
  rock: 'from-rose-600 to-purple-950',
  electronic: 'from-fuchsia-600 to-pink-950',
  pop: 'from-pink-500 to-purple-900',
  metal: 'from-purple-800 to-pink-950',
  indie: 'from-pink-400 to-rose-900',
  rnb: 'from-rose-400 to-purple-900',
  dance: 'from-pink-500 to-rose-900',
};

const itemKey = (i: ExternalItem) => `${i.artist}|${i.title}`;

export default function BrowsePage() {
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const canDownload = user?.role === 'ADMIN' || Boolean(user?.canUpload);
  const [meta, setMeta] = useState<DiscoverMeta | null>(null);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('top');
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [data, setData] = useState<DiscoverResponse | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [hint, setHint] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const reqId = useRef(0);

  const current = usePlayer((s) => s.queue[s.index]);
  const playing = usePlayer((s) => s.playing);

  useEffect(() => {
    api<DiscoverMeta>('/discover/meta')
      .then(setMeta)
      .catch(() => undefined);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab === 'genre' && !genre) {
      setData(null);
      setError(null);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    const id = ++reqId.current;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const fetchItunesFallback = async (genreTag?: string) => {
      const term = genreTag ? genreTag : 'top songs';
      const res = await fetch(`https://itunes.apple.com/search?term=${encodeURIComponent(term)}&entity=song&limit=50`);
      const raw = await res.json();
      const items: ExternalItem[] = (raw.results || []).map((r: any) => ({
        artist: r.artistName,
        title: r.trackName,
        album: r.collectionName,
        coverUrl: r.artworkUrl100?.replace('100x100bb', '600x600bb'),
        previewUrl: r.previewUrl,
        externalUrl: r.trackViewUrl,
        source: 'lastfm' as const,
        local: null,
        downloadStatus: null,
      }));
      return { kind: tab === 'top' ? ('chart' as const) : ('genre' as const), source: 'lastfm' as const, items };
    };

    const path = tab === 'top' ? '/discover/charts' : `/discover/genre/${genre}`;

    api<DiscoverResponse>(path, { query: { limit: 50 } })
      .then((r) => {
        if (id !== reqId.current) return;
        if (!r.items || r.items.length === 0) {
          fetchItunesFallback(genre || undefined).then((res) => id === reqId.current && setData(res));
        } else {
          setData(r);
        }
      })
      .catch(async () => {
        if (id !== reqId.current) return;
        try {
          const fallbackData = await fetchItunesFallback(genre || undefined);
          if (id === reqId.current) setData(fallbackData);
        } catch (err) {
          if (id === reqId.current) setError('Не удалось подгрузить мировые чарты.');
        }
      })
      .finally(() => id === reqId.current && setLoading(false));
  }, [tab, genre]);

  const patchItem = (item: ExternalItem, p: Partial<ExternalItem>) =>
    setData((d) => d && { ...d, items: (d.items || []).map((i) => (itemKey(i) === itemKey(item) ? { ...i, ...p } : i)) });

  const play = async (item: ExternalItem) => {
    setHint(null);
    const player = usePlayer.getState();

    const isSamePreview = current?.kind === 'preview' && current.src === item.previewUrl;
    const isSameLocal = item.local && current?.kind === 'local' && current.id === item.local.trackId;

    if (isSamePreview || isSameLocal) {
      player.toggle();
      return;
    }

    if (item.local) {
      const q: NewQueueItem = {
        kind: 'local',
        id: item.local.trackId,
        title: item.title,
        artist: item.artist,
        coverUrl: item.local.coverUrl ?? item.coverUrl,
        duration: item.local.duration,
      };
      return player.playQueue([q]);
    }

    setBusy(itemKey(item));
    try {
      let previewUrl = item.previewUrl;
      let coverUrl = item.coverUrl;
      if (!previewUrl) {
        const r = await api<{ previewUrl: string | null; coverUrl: string | null }>('/discover/preview', {
          query: { artist: item.artist, title: item.title },
        });
        previewUrl = r.previewUrl ?? undefined;
        coverUrl ??= r.coverUrl ?? undefined;
      }
      if (previewUrl) {
        player.playQueue([{ kind: 'preview', title: item.title, artist: item.artist, coverUrl, src: previewUrl, duration: 30 }]);
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      /* fallback */
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
    setHint(`Превью для «${item.title}» нет — открыт поиск на YouTube.`);
    window.open(youtubeSearchUrl(item.artist, item.title), '_blank', 'noopener');
  };

  const download = async (item: ExternalItem) => {
    setHint(null);
    if (!canDownload) {
      setHint('Загрузка в медиатеку доступна администратору и пользователям с правом загрузки.');
      return;
    }
    setBusy(itemKey(item));
    try {
      patchItem(item, { downloadStatus: 'PENDING' });
      const r = await api<{ status: 'PENDING' | 'PROCESSING' }>('/discover/download', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: {
          artist: item.artist.trim(),
          title: item.title.trim(),
          coverUrl: item.coverUrl ?? null,
          source: 'lastfm',
        },
      });
      patchItem(item, { downloadStatus: r.status });
      setHint(`«${item.title}» передан бэкенду в очередь на скачивание.`);
    } catch (e) {
      patchItem(item, { downloadStatus: null });
      if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === 'already_local') setHint('Этот трек уже есть в вашей медиатеке.');
      else setHint((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const genresList = meta?.genres && meta.genres.length > 0 ? meta.genres : DEFAULT_GENRES;
  const genreLabel = genresList.find((g) => g.tag === genre)?.label ?? genre;
  const items = data?.items ?? [];
  const localCount = items.filter((i) => i.local).length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5 pb-20">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-300 via-rose-300 to-purple-300 bg-clip-text text-transparent">Обзор популярного</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-pink-300/60">
          Самые популярные мировые треки и жанровые топ-50. Треки из вашей медиатеки играют полностью, а новые можно моментально скачивать.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/40 p-1.5 backdrop-blur-xl">
          {TABS.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setTab(t.id);
                if (t.id === 'top') setGenre(null);
              }}
              className={cn(
                'rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer',
                tab === t.id
                  ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 via-rose-400 to-purple-400 text-pink-950 shadow-md'
                  : 'text-pink-300/70 hover:text-pink-100'
              )}
            >
              {t.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {tab === 'genre' && !genre && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {genresList.map((g) => (
            <button
              key={g.tag}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setGenre(g.tag)}
              className={cn(
                'flex h-32 flex-col justify-end rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br p-4 text-left shadow-lg border border-pink-500/20 transition-transform hover:scale-[1.02] cursor-pointer',
                TONES[g.tag] ?? 'from-pink-900 to-purple-950'
              )}
            >
              <span className="text-lg font-bold text-pink-100">{g.label}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-pink-300/70">Топ-50 {g.label}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'genre' && genre && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/40 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-200 hover:bg-pink-900/40 cursor-pointer"
            onClick={() => setGenre(null)}
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Все жанры
          </button>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-pink-100">Топ-50: {genreLabel}</h2>
        </div>
      )}

      {data && (tab !== 'genre' || genre) && (
        <p className="text-xs text-pink-300/50">
          {tab === 'top' ? 'Главный мировой Топ-50 треков за неделю' : `Топ-50 жанра «${genreLabel}»`}
          {' · '}в вашей медиатеке: {localCount} из {items.length}
        </p>
      )}
      {hint && <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 p-3 text-xs text-amber-200">{hint}</div>}
      {error && <div className="rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 p-3 text-xs text-rose-200">{error}</div>}

      {(tab !== 'genre' || genre) && (
        <div className={cn('rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/20 backdrop-blur-md overflow-hidden divide-y divide-pink-500/10', loading && 'opacity-60')}>
          {loading && <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-xs text-pink-300/50">Загрузка самых популярных треков…</div>}

          {!loading && items.map((item, i) => {
            const k = itemKey(item);
            const isCurrent = item.local
              ? current?.kind === 'local' && current.id === item.local.trackId
              : current?.kind === 'preview' && current.src === item.previewUrl;
            const queued = item.downloadStatus === 'PENDING' || item.downloadStatus === 'PROCESSING';
            return (
              <div key={`${k}-${i}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-pink-900/20 transition-colors">
                <span className="w-6 text-right text-xs font-semibold tabular-nums text-pink-400/40">{i + 1}</span>
                <button type="button" className="relative shrink-0 cursor-pointer group" onClick={() => void play(item)} disabled={busy === k} aria-label="Играть">
                  <Cover url={item.local?.coverUrl ?? item.coverUrl} title={item.title} size={44} className="rounded-xl shadow-md" />
                  <span className={cn(
                    "absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-pink-950/60 transition-opacity",
                    isCurrent ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
                  )}>
                    {isCurrent && playing ? <Pause size={18} fill="white" /> : <Play size={18} fill="white" />}
                  </span>
                </button>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className={cn('truncate text-sm font-semibold', isCurrent ? 'text-pink-300' : 'text-pink-100')}>{item.title}</div>
                  <div className="truncate text-xs text-pink-300/60">{item.artist}</div>
                </div>

                {item.local ? (
                  <span className="hidden shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-pink-500/20 border border-pink-400/30 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-pink-200 sm:flex font-medium">
                    <Check size={14} /> В медиатеке{item.local.duration ? ` · ${formatDuration(item.local.duration)}` : ''}
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <a
                      className="rounded-xl p-2 text-pink-300/50 hover:text-pink-100 hover:bg-pink-900/30 transition-colors"
                      href={youtubeSearchUrl(item.artist, item.title)}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      title="Искать на YouTube"
                    >
                      <Youtube size={16} />
                    </a>
                    {item.externalUrl && (
                      <a
                        className="rounded-xl p-2 text-pink-300/50 hover:text-pink-100 hover:bg-pink-900/30 transition-colors"
                        href={item.externalUrl}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noopener noreferrer"
                        title="Открыть в оригинале"
                      >
                        <ExternalLink size={16} />
                      </a>
                    )}
                    {queued ? (
                      <span className="shrink-0 rounded-xl bg-pink-900/40 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-300 border border-pink-500/20">
                        {item.downloadStatus === 'PROCESSING' ? 'Скачивается' : 'В очереди'}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 via-rose-400 to-purple-400 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-pink-950 shadow-md hover:opacity-95 cursor-pointer"
                        onClick={() => void download(item)}
                        disabled={busy === k}
                      >
                        <Download size={14} /> В медиатеку
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/library/page.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Check,
  Heart,
  MoreVertical,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Plus,
  Shuffle,
  Trash2,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Cover } from '@/components/Cover';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { Track } from '@/lib/types';
import { cn, formatDuration } from '@/lib/utils';
import { useAuth } from '@/store/auth';
import { usePlayer, type NewQueueItem } from '@/store/player';

interface Playlist {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string | null;
  trackCount?: number;
}

type SortOption = 'recent' | 'title' | 'artist' | 'year';

export default function LibraryPage() {
  const user = useAuth((s) => s.user);
  const canManage = user?.role === 'ADMIN' || Boolean(user?.canUpload);

  const [tracks, setTracks] = useState<Track[]>([]);
  const [playlists, setPlaylists] = useState<Playlist[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Фильтры и поиск
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [genre, setGenre] = useState<string>('all');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortOption>('recent');

  // Мультивыбор
  const [selecting, setSelecting] = useState(false);
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Контекстное меню для отдельного трека
  const [activeMenuId, setActiveMenuId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Модальные окна
  const [addToPlaylistTrackIds, setAddToPlaylistTrackIds] = useState<string[] | null>(null);
  const [deleteTrackIds, setDeleteTrackIds] = useState<string[] | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const current = usePlayer((s) => s.queue[s.index]);
  const playing = usePlayer((s) => s.playing);

  const loadData = async () => {
    try {
      const [tRes, pRes] = await Promise.all([
        api<{ items: Track[] }>('/tracks'),
        api<{ items: Playlist[] }>('/playlists').catch(() => ({ items: [] })),
      ]);
      setTracks(tRes.items || []);
      setPlaylists(pRes.items || []);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, []);

  // Закрываем контекстное меню при клике вне его
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setActiveMenuId(null);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  // Список уникальных жанров
  const genres = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    tracks.forEach((t) => t.genres?.forEach((g) => set.add(g.name)));
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [tracks]);

  // Фильтрация и сортировка
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return tracks
      .filter((t) => {
        const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();
        const matchesQuery =
          !q || t.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.artist.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchesGenre =
          genre === 'all' || t.genres?.some((g) => g.name === genre);
        return matchesQuery && matchesGenre;
      })
      .sort((a, b) => {
        if (sort === 'title') return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
        if (sort === 'artist') return a.artist.localeCompare(b.artist);
        if (sort === 'year') return (b.year ?? 0) - (a.year ?? 0);
        return new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt).getTime();
      });
  }, [tracks, query, genre, sort]);

  const toggleSelect = (id: string) => {
    const next = new Set(selectedIds);
    if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
    else next.add(id);
    setSelectedIds(next);
  };

  const toggleSelectAll = () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === filtered.length) {
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
    } else {
      setSelectedIds(new Set(filtered.map((t) => t.id)));
    }
  };

  const playTrack = (track: Track) => {
    const player = usePlayer.getState();
    if (current?.kind === 'local' && current.id === track.id) {
      player.toggle();
      return;
    }
    const queueItems: NewQueueItem[] = filtered.map((t) => ({
      kind: 'local',
      id: t.id,
      title: t.title,
      artist: t.artist,
      coverUrl: t.coverUrl,
      duration: t.duration,
    }));
    const index = filtered.findIndex((t) => t.id === track.id);
    player.playQueue(queueItems, index >= 0 ? index : 0);
  };

  const playShuffle = () => {
    if (filtered.length === 0) return;
    const shuffled = [...filtered].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    const queueItems: NewQueueItem[] = shuffled.map((t) => ({
      kind: 'local',
      id: t.id,
      title: t.title,
      artist: t.artist,
      coverUrl: t.coverUrl,
      duration: t.duration,
    }));
    usePlayer.getState().playQueue(queueItems, 0);
  };

  const toggleFavorite = async (track: Track) => {
    const isFav = track.isFavorite;
    setTracks((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => (t.id === track.id ? { ...t, isFavorite: !isFav } : t))
    );
    try {
      if (isFav) {
        await api(`/favorites/${track.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      } else {
        await api(`/favorites/${track.id}`, { method: 'POST' });
      }
    } catch {
      setTracks((prev) =>
        prev.map((t) => (t.id === track.id ? { ...t, isFavorite: isFav } : t))
      );
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!deleteTrackIds || deleteTrackIds.length === 0) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await Promise.all(
        deleteTrackIds.map((id) => api(`/tracks/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }))
      );
      setTracks((prev) => prev.filter((t) => !deleteTrackIds.includes(t.id)));
      setSelectedIds((prev) => {
        const next = new Set(prev);
        deleteTrackIds.forEach((id) => next.delete(id));
        return next;
      });
      setDeleteTrackIds(null);
    } catch (e) {
      alert(`Ошибка при удалении: ${(e as Error).message}`);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAddToPlaylist = async (playlistId: string) => {
    if (!addToPlaylistTrackIds || addToPlaylistTrackIds.length === 0) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await Promise.all(
        addToPlaylistTrackIds.map((trackId) =>
          api(`/playlists/${playlistId}/tracks`, {
            method: 'POST',
            body: { trackId },
          })
        )
      );
      setAddToPlaylistTrackIds(null);
    } catch (e) {
      alert(`Ошибка добавления в плейлист: ${(e as Error).message}`);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5 pb-24">
      {/* Шапка */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-300 via-rose-300 to-purple-300 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
            Библиотека
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-pink-300/60">{tracks.length} треков</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setSelecting(!selecting);
              setSelectedIds(new Set());
            }}
            className={cn(
              'rounded-xl border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-all cursor-pointer',
              selecting
                ? 'border-pink-400 bg-pink-500/20 text-pink-200'
                : 'border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/40 text-pink-300 hover:text-pink-100'
            )}
          >
            {selecting ? 'Отменить выбор' : 'Выбрать несколько'}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={playShuffle}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 via-rose-400 to-purple-400 px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-pink-950 shadow-md hover:opacity-95 cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Shuffle size={14} /> Играть вперемешку
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Панель массовых действий */}
      {selecting && selectedIds.size > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/30 bg-pink-950/60 p-3 text-xs backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={toggleSelectAll}
              className="text-pink-300 hover:text-pink-100 underline cursor-pointer"
            >
              {selectedIds.size === filtered.length ? 'Снять выделение' : 'Выбрать все'}
            </button>
            <span className="text-pink-200 font-semibold">
              Выбрано: {selectedIds.size}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAddToPlaylistTrackIds(Array.from(selectedIds))}
              className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl border border-pink-500/30 bg-pink-900/40 px-3 py-1 text-pink-200 hover:bg-pink-800/40 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus size={14} /> В плейлист
            </button>
            {canManage && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDeleteTrackIds(Array.from(selectedIds))}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-rose-600/80 px-3 py-1 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-rose-500 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Trash2 size={14} /> Удалить
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Фильтры и поиск */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Поиск по исполнителю и названию..."
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          className="min-w-[220px] flex-1 rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/40 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-pink-100 placeholder-pink-400/40 outline-none focus:border-pink-400"
        />

        <select
          value={genre}
          onChange={(e) => setGenre(e.target.value)}
          className="rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/60 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-100 outline-none cursor-pointer"
        >
          <option value="all" className="bg-pink-950">
            Все жанры
          </option>
          {genres.map((g) => (
            <option key={g} value={g} className="bg-pink-950">
              {g}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <select
          value={sort}
          onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as SortOption)}
          className="rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/60 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-100 outline-none cursor-pointer"
        >
          <option value="recent" className="bg-pink-950">
            Недавно добавленные
          </option>
          <option value="title" className="bg-pink-950">
            По названию
          </option>
          <option value="artist" className="bg-pink-950">
            По исполнителю
          </option>
          <option value="year" className="bg-pink-950">
            По году
          </option>
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* Список треков */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/15 bg-pink-950/20 backdrop-blur-md overflow-visible divide-y divide-pink-500/10">
        {loading && (
          <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-xs text-pink-300/50">
            Загрузка медиатеки...
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && filtered.length === 0 && (
          <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-xs text-pink-300/50">
            Треки не найдены
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading &&
          filtered.map((track, i) => {
            const isCurrent = current?.kind === 'local' && current.id === track.id;
            const isSelected = selectedIds.has(track.id);
            const isMenuOpen = activeMenuId === track.id;

            return (
              <div
                key={track.id}
                className={cn(
                  'relative flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 hover:bg-pink-900/20 transition-colors group',
                  isSelected && 'bg-pink-500/10'
                )}
              >
                {/* Чекбокс мультивыбора */}
                {selecting ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleSelect(track.id)}
                    className={cn(
                      'flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border transition-colors cursor-pointer',
                      isSelected
                        ? 'border-pink-400 bg-pink-500 text-pink-950'
                        : 'border-pink-500/30 bg-pink-950/40'
                    )}
                  >
                    {isSelected && <Check size={12} strokeWidth={3} />}
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <span className="w-6 text-right text-xs font-semibold tabular-nums text-pink-400/40">
                    {i + 1}
                  </span>
                )}

                {/* Постер / Play */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => playTrack(track)}
                  className="relative shrink-0 cursor-pointer group/cover"
                >
                  <Cover
                    url={track.coverUrl}
                    title={track.title}
                    size={40}
                    className="rounded-xl shadow-md"
                  />
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      'absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-pink-950/60 transition-opacity',
                      isCurrent ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0 group-hover/cover:opacity-100'
                    )}
                  >
                    {isCurrent && playing ? (
                      <Pause size={16} fill="white" />
                    ) : (
                      <Play size={16} fill="white" />
                    )}
                  </span>
                </button>

                {/* Название и Артист */}
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div
                    className={cn(
                      'truncate text-sm font-semibold',
                      isCurrent ? 'text-pink-300' : 'text-pink-100'
                    )}
                  >
                    {track.title}
                  </div>
                  <div className="truncate text-xs text-pink-300/60">
                    {track.artist}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Избранное */}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void toggleFavorite(track)}
                  className="p-1.5 text-pink-300/40 hover:text-pink-400 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Heart
                    size={16}
                    className={cn(track.isFavorite && 'fill-pink-500 text-pink-500')}
                  />
                </button>

                {/* Длительность */}
                <span className="w-12 text-right text-xs tabular-nums text-pink-300/50">
                  {formatDuration(track.duration)}
                </span>

                {/* Меню трех точек */}
                <div className="relative">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveMenuId(isMenuOpen ? null : track.id)}
                    className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-pink-300/40 hover:text-pink-100 hover:bg-pink-900/30 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <MoreVertical size={16} />
                  </button>

                  {isMenuOpen && (
                    <div
                      ref={menuRef}
                      className="absolute right-0 top-full z-50 mt-1 min-w-[180px] rounded-xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/95 p-1.5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95"
                    >
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setActiveMenuId(null);
                          setAddToPlaylistTrackIds([track.id]);
                        }}
                        className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-200 hover:bg-pink-900/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left"
                      >
                        <Plus size={14} /> Добавить в плейлист
                      </button>

                      {canManage && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setActiveMenuId(null);
                            setDeleteTrackIds([track.id]);
                          }}
                          className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300 hover:bg-rose-950/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left"
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={14} /> Удалить из медиатеки
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
      </div>

      {/* Модальное окно "Добавить в плейлист" */}
      {Boolean(addToPlaylistTrackIds) && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/90 p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-pink-500/15 pb-3">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-pink-100">Добавить в плейлист</h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setAddToPlaylistTrackIds(null)}
                className="text-pink-300/50 hover:text-pink-100 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="max-h-60 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pr-1">
              {playlists.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs text-pink-300/60 py-4 text-center">
                  У вас пока нет созданных плейлистов.
                </p>
              ) : (
                playlists.map((pl) => (
                  <button
                    key={pl.id}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={busy}
                    onClick={() => void handleAddToPlaylist(pl.id)}
                    className="flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/40 px-4 py-3 text-left hover:bg-pink-900/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-pink-100">
                        {pl.name}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-pink-300/50">
                        {pl.trackCount ?? 0} треков
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <Plus size={16} className="text-pink-400" />
                  </button>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Модальное окно подтверждения удаления */}
      {Boolean(deleteTrackIds) && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/20 bg-pink-950/90 p-5 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-xl space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-pink-500/15 pb-3">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-rose-200">Удалить из медиатеки?</h3>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDeleteTrackIds(null)}
                className="text-pink-300/50 hover:text-pink-100 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <p className="text-sm text-pink-200">
              Вы действительно хотите удалить {deleteTrackIds?.length} трек(ов) из вашей медиатеки? Файлы будут удалены с сервера.
            </p>

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDeleteTrackIds(null)}
                className="rounded-xl border border-pink-500/20 bg-pink-950/40 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-pink-300 hover:bg-pink-900/40 cursor-pointer"
              >
                Отмена
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={busy}
                onClick={() => void handleDelete()}
                className="rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-rose-500 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {busy ? 'Удаление...' : 'Удалить'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/playlists/page.tsx ---

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Import, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { Cover } from '@/components/Cover';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import type { PlaylistSummary } from '@/lib/types';

export default function PlaylistsPage() {
  const [mine, setMine] = useState<PlaylistSummary[] | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const load = () => {
    api<{ items: PlaylistSummary[] }>('/playlists')
      .then((r) => setMine(r.items))
      .catch((e: Error) => setError(e.message));
  };
  useEffect(load, []);

  const create = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim()) return;
    try {
      await api('/playlists', { method: 'POST', body: { name: name.trim() } });
      setName('');
      load();
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Плейлисты</h1>
        <Link href="/import" className="btn">
          <Import size={16} /> Импорт из Spotify
        </Link>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={create} className="card flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-3">
        <input className="input !w-auto min-w-0 flex-1" placeholder="Название нового плейлиста" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} maxLength={100} />
        <button className="btn btn-primary" disabled={!name.trim()}>
          <Plus size={16} /> Создать
        </button>
      </form>
      {error && <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}

      {mine === null ? (
        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>
      ) : mine.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Пока нет плейлистов.</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5">
          {mine.map((p) => (
            <Link key={p.id} href={`/playlists/${p.id}`} className="card block p-3 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-900">
              <Cover url={p.coverUrl} title={p.name} size={160} className="!h-auto aspect-square !w-full" />
              <div className="mt-3 truncate text-sm font-medium">{p.name}</div>
              <div className="truncate text-xs text-zinc-500">{p.trackCount} треков</div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/playlists/[id]/PlaylistClient.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { Music, Play, Trash2, ArrowLeft, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';

interface Track {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  artist: string;
  duration: number;
  coverUrl?: string;
}

interface Playlist {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  isPublic: boolean;
  isMine: boolean;
  owner: string;
  tracks: Track[];
}

export default function PlaylistClient() {
  const params = useParams();
  const id = params?.id as string;

  const [playlist, setPlaylist] = useState<Playlist | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id || id === 'undefined') {
      setError('Некорректный ID плейлиста');
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    const fetchPlaylist = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        const data = await api<Playlist>(`/playlists/${id}`);
        setPlaylist(data);
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Failed to load playlist:', err);
        setError(err?.message || 'Не удалось загрузить плейлист');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    fetchPlaylist();
  }, [id]);

  const handleDeleteTrack = async (trackId: string) => {
    try {
      await api(`/playlists/${id}/tracks/${trackId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      setPlaylist((prev) =>
        prev
          ? {
              ...prev,
              tracks: prev.tracks.filter((t) => t.id !== trackId),
            }
          : null
      );
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to remove track:', err);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-full">
        <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-pink-500 animate-spin" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !playlist) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-4 p-6">
        <p className="text-rose-400 text-lg font-medium">{error || 'Плейлист не найден'}</p>
        <Link
          href="/playlists"
          className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-pink-500 hover:bg-pink-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition-all font-medium"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={18} /> К списку плейлистов
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto overflow-y-auto">
      <Link
        href="/playlists"
        className="flex items-center gap-2 text-pink-300/70 hover:text-pink-200 mb-6 w-fit transition-all text-sm font-medium"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Назад к плейлистам
      </Link>

      <div className="flex items-end gap-6 mb-8 bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-950/40 to-rose-950/20 p-6 rounded-3xl border border-pink-500/15">
        <div className="w-32 h-32 rounded-2xl bg-pink-900/40 border border-pink-500/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-lg">
          <Music size={48} className="text-pink-400" />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-pink-400 font-bold">Плейлист</span>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-pink-100">{playlist.name}</h1>
          {playlist.description && <p className="text-sm text-pink-200/70">{playlist.description}</p>}
          <p className="text-xs text-pink-300/50">Треков: {playlist.tracks.length} • Автор: {playlist.owner}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        {playlist.tracks.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-12 text-pink-300/50 border border-dashed border-pink-500/20 rounded-2xl">
            В этом плейлисте пока нет треков. Добавьте их из общей библиотеки!
          </div>
        ) : (
          playlist.tracks.map((track, index) => (
            <div
              key={track.id}
              className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-pink-950/20 border border-pink-500/10 hover:border-pink-500/30 transition-all group"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 truncate">
                <span className="text-sm text-pink-400/60 w-6 text-right font-mono">{index + 1}</span>
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-pink-900/30 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 overflow-hidden">
                  {track.coverUrl ? (
                    <img src={track.coverUrl} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <Music size={18} className="text-pink-400" />
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="truncate">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-pink-100 truncate">{track.title}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-pink-300/60 truncate">{track.artist}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {playlist.isMine && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleDeleteTrack(track.id)}
                  className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 text-pink-400/60 hover:text-rose-400 p-2 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                  title="Удалить из плейлиста"
                >
                  <Trash2 size={18} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/playlists/[id]/page.tsx ---

import PlaylistClient from './PlaylistClient';

export const dynamic = 'force-static';
export const dynamicParams = false;

export default function Page() {
  return <PlaylistClient />;
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/import/page.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { FileText, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

/* ---------------- Типы ---------------- */

interface ImportTrack {
  artist: string;
  title: string;
}
interface ImportResult {
  playlist: { id: string; name: string };
  total: number;
  matched: number;
  missing: ImportTrack[];
}
interface SpPlaylist {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  total: number;
}

/* ---------------- Spotify: OAuth Authorization Code + PKCE (всё происходит в браузере) ---------------- */

const SCOPES = 'playlist-read-private playlist-read-collaborative';
const TOKEN_KEY = 'loral-sp-token';
const VERIFIER_KEY = 'loral-sp-verifier';
const STATE_KEY = 'loral-sp-state';
const PENDING_KEY = 'loral-pending-import';

const ALPHABET = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789-._~';

function randomString(len = 64): string {
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(len));
  return Array.from(bytes, (b) => ALPHABET[b % ALPHABET.length]).join('');
}

async function challengeFor(verifier: string): Promise<string> {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(verifier));
  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(digest)))
    .replace(/\+/g, '-')
    .replace(/\//g, '_')
    .replace(/=+$/, '');
}

class SpotifyError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public status: number,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

function loadToken(): string | null {
  try {
    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const { token, exp } = JSON.parse(raw) as { token: string; exp: number };
    return Date.now() < exp ? token : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function spotify<T>(token: string, urlOrPath: string): Promise<T> {
  const url = urlOrPath.startsWith('http') ? urlOrPath : `https://api.spotify.com/v1${urlOrPath}`;
  const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (res.ok) return (await res.json()) as T;
  const body = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as { error?: { message?: string } } | null;
  throw new SpotifyError(res.status, body?.error?.message ?? `Ошибка Spotify ${res.status}`);
}

interface Paging<T> {
  items: T[];
  next: string | null;
}

async function fetchAll<T>(token: string, firstUrl: string, limit = 5000): Promise<T[]> {
  const out: T[] = [];
  let next: string | null = firstUrl;
  while (next && out.length < limit) {
    const page: Paging<T> = await spotify<Paging<T>>(token, next);
    out.push(...page.items);
    next = page.next;
  }
  return out;
}

interface RawPlaylist {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  // Spotify переименовал tracks → items (февраль 2026); поддерживаем оба варианта
  items?: { total?: number };
  tracks?: { total?: number };
}
interface RawEntry {
  item?: RawTrack | null;
  track?: RawTrack | null;
}
interface RawTrack {
  name?: string;
  type?: string;
  artists?: { name: string }[];
}

async function fetchPlaylistTracks(token: string, id: string): Promise<ImportTrack[]> {
  let entries: RawEntry[];
  try {
    entries = await fetchAll<RawEntry>(token, `/playlists/${id}/items?limit=100&additional_types=track`);
  } catch (e) {
    if (!(e instanceof SpotifyError) || e.status !== 404) throw e;
    entries = await fetchAll<RawEntry>(token, `/playlists/${id}/tracks?limit=100`); // старое название эндпоинта
  }
  const out: ImportTrack[] = [];
  for (const e of entries) {
    const t = e.item ?? e.track;
    if (!t?.name || (t.type && t.type !== 'track')) continue; // пропускаем подкасты и пустые записи
    const artist = t.artists?.[0]?.name;
    if (artist) out.push({ artist, title: t.name });
  }
  return out;
}

/* ---------------- Страница ---------------- */

export default function ImportPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'spotify' | 'file'>('spotify');
  const [clientId, setClientId] = useState<string | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [redirectUri, setRedirectUri] = useState('');
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [playlists, setPlaylists] = useState<SpPlaylist[] | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // файл / текст
  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState('');
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [plName, setPlName] = useState('');

  const isLocalhost = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.location.hostname === 'localhost';

  const logoutSpotify = useCallback(() => {
    sessionStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    setToken(null);
    setPlaylists(null);
  }, []);

  const loadPlaylists = useCallback(
    async (tk: string) => {
      setBusy('Загружаю ваши плейлисты из Spotify…');
      try {
        const raw = await fetchAll<RawPlaylist>(tk, '/me/playlists?limit=50');
        setPlaylists(raw.filter(Boolean).map((p) => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name, total: p.items?.total ?? p.tracks?.total ?? 0 })));
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 401) {
          logoutSpotify();
          setError('Сессия Spotify истекла. Войдите снова.');
        } else if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 403) {
          setError(
            `Spotify отказал в доступе (${e.message}). Приложение в режиме разработки доступно только добавленным пользователям — добавьте свой аккаунт в User Management в панели разработчика Spotify.`,
          );
        } else setError((e as Error).message);
      } finally {
        setBusy(null);
      }
    },
    [logoutSpotify],
  );

  // Инициализация: настройки сервера, возврат из Spotify с ?code=…
  useEffect(() => {
    setRedirectUri(`${window.location.origin}/import`);
    let cancelled = false;

    (async () => {
      const cfg = await api<{ spotifyClientId: string | null }>('/import/config').catch(() => ({ spotifyClientId: null }));
      if (cancelled) return;
      setClientId(cfg.spotifyClientId);

      // параметры возврата: из адресной строки или сохранённые, если пользователь успел залогиниться в Loral
      const search = sessionStorage.getItem(PENDING_KEY) ?? window.location.search;
      sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_KEY);
      const params = new URLSearchParams(search);
      const code = params.get('code');
      const spError = params.get('error');
      if (code || spError) window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/import');

      if (spError) {
        setError(spError === 'access_denied' ? 'Вы отклонили доступ в Spotify.' : `Spotify: ${spError}`);
        return;
      }
      if (code && cfg.spotifyClientId) {
        setBusy('Завершаю вход в Spotify…');
        try {
          if (params.get('state') !== sessionStorage.getItem(STATE_KEY)) throw new Error('Не совпал параметр state — начните вход заново.');
          const verifier = sessionStorage.getItem(VERIFIER_KEY);
          if (!verifier) throw new Error('Потерян code_verifier — начните вход заново.');
          const res = await fetch('https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
            body: new URLSearchParams({
              client_id: cfg.spotifyClientId,
              grant_type: 'authorization_code',
              code,
              redirect_uri: `${window.location.origin}/import`,
              code_verifier: verifier,
            }),
          });
          const data = (await res.json()) as { access_token?: string; expires_in?: number; error_description?: string };
          if (!res.ok || !data.access_token) throw new Error(data.error_description ?? 'Spotify не выдал токен');
          sessionStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, JSON.stringify({ token: data.access_token, exp: Date.now() + (data.expires_in ?? 3600) * 1000 - 30_000 }));
          sessionStorage.removeItem(VERIFIER_KEY);
          sessionStorage.removeItem(STATE_KEY);
          if (!cancelled) {
            setToken(data.access_token);
            await loadPlaylists(data.access_token);
          }
        } catch (e) {
          if (!cancelled) setError((e as Error).message);
        } finally {
          if (!cancelled) setBusy(null);
        }
        return;
      }

      const saved = loadToken();
      if (saved) {
        setToken(saved);
        void loadPlaylists(saved);
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [loadPlaylists]);

  const connect = async () => {
    if (!clientId) return;
    setError(null);
    const verifier = randomString(64);
    const state = randomString(16);
    sessionStorage.setItem(VERIFIER_KEY, verifier);
    sessionStorage.setItem(STATE_KEY, state);
    const url = new URL('https://accounts.spotify.com/authorize');
    url.search = new URLSearchParams({
      client_id: clientId,
      response_type: 'code',
      redirect_uri: redirectUri,
      scope: SCOPES,
      state,
      code_challenge_method: 'S256',
      code_challenge: await challengeFor(verifier),
    }).toString();
    window.location.href = url.toString();
  };

  const importFromSpotify = async (p: SpPlaylist) => {
    if (!token) return;
    setError(null);
    setBusy(`Читаю треки «${p.name}»…`);
    try {
      const tracks = await fetchPlaylistTracks(token, p.id);
      if (tracks.length === 0) throw new Error('В плейлисте не нашлось треков.');
      setBusy('Сопоставляю с вашей медиатекой…');
      const created = await api<ImportResult>('/import/playlist', { method: 'POST', body: { name: p.name, tracks } });
      router.push(`/playlists/${created.playlist.id}`);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 401) {
        logoutSpotify();
        setError('Сессия Spotify истекла. Войдите снова.');
      } else if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 403) {
        setError('Spotify не отдаёт содержимое этого плейлиста: доступны только плейлисты, которые вы создали или ведёте совместно. Для остальных используйте импорт из CSV (вкладка «Файл или текст»).');
      } else if (e instanceof SpotifyError && e.status === 429) {
        setError('Spotify ограничил число запросов. Подождите минуту и повторите.');
      } else setError((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const onFile = async (f: File | undefined) => {
    if (!f) return;
    setFileName(f.name);
    setText(await f.text());
    if (!plName) setPlName(f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ''));
  };

  const importFromText = async () => {
    setError(null);
    setBusy('Импортирую…');
    try {
      const created = await api<ImportResult>('/import/playlist', { method: 'POST', body: { name: plName.trim() || 'Импортированный плейлист', text } });
      router.push(`/playlists/${created.playlist.id}`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError((e as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Импорт плейлиста</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500">
          Loral создаст плейлист сразу. Треки, которых нет в медиатеке, скачиваются в фоне и появляются в плейлисте по мере готовности.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-lg bg-zinc-900 p-1 text-sm w-fit">
        {(
          [
            ['spotify', 'Spotify'],
            ['file', 'Файл или текст'],
          ] as const
        ).map(([id, label]) => (
          <button key={id} onClick={() => setTab(id)} className={cn('rounded-md px-4 py-1.5', tab === id ? 'bg-accent font-semibold text-black' : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white')}>
            {label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {error && <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-300">{error}</div>}
      {busy && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">
          <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin text-accent" /> {busy}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'spotify' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {clientId === undefined && <div className="text-sm text-zinc-500">Загрузка…</div>}

          {clientId === null && (
            <div className="card space-y-3 p-5 text-sm text-zinc-300">
              <div className="font-semibold text-white">Вход через Spotify ещё не настроен</div>
              <ol className="list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5">
                <li>
                  Откройте <b>developer.spotify.com/dashboard</b> и создайте приложение (потребуется аккаунт Spotify Premium).
                </li>
                <li>
                  В поле <b>Redirect URI</b> укажите: <code className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-yellow-300">{redirectUri || 'http://127.0.0.1:3000/import'}</code>
                </li>
                <li>
                  Скопируйте <b>Client ID</b> в <code className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5">backend/.env</code> (<code>SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID=…</code>) и перезапустите сервер.
                </li>
              </ol>
              <p className="text-zinc-500">Без настройки можно импортировать плейлист из CSV-файла — вкладка «Файл или текст».</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {clientId && !token && !busy && (
            <div className="card space-y-4 p-5">
              {isLocalhost && (
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-200">
                  Spotify не принимает адрес <b>localhost</b>. Откройте Loral как <b>http://127.0.0.1:3000</b> (войдите там заново) и повторите.
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="text-sm text-zinc-300">
                Loral запросит у Spotify доступ только на чтение ваших плейлистов. Токен хранится в браузере до закрытия вкладки и на сервер не отправляется.
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                Redirect URI в настройках вашего Spotify-приложения должен быть: <code className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-yellow-300">{redirectUri}</code>
              </div>
              <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={connect} disabled={isLocalhost}>
                Войти через Spotify
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {token && playlists && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
                <span className="text-zinc-400">Ваши плейлисты в Spotify ({playlists.length})</span>
                <button className="text-zinc-500 hover:text-white" onClick={logoutSpotify}>
                  Выйти из Spotify
                </button>
              </div>
              {playlists.length === 0 && <div className="card p-6 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Плейлистов не найдено.</div>}
              <ul className="card divide-y divide-white/5">
                {playlists.map((p) => (
                  <li key={p.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="truncate text-sm">{p.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{p.total} треков</div>
                    </div>
                    <button className="btn shrink-0" onClick={() => void importFromSpotify(p)} disabled={!!busy}>
                      Перенести
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'file' && (
        <div className="card space-y-4 p-5">
          <div className="space-y-2 text-sm text-zinc-300">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-white">
              <FileText size={16} className="text-accent" /> Как получить список из Spotify без входа
            </div>
            <ol className="list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5">
              <li>
                Откройте <b>exportify.net</b>, войдите через Spotify и нажмите «Export» у нужного плейлиста — скачается CSV.
              </li>
              <li>Загрузите этот файл сюда. Подойдёт и обычный текстовый список, по строке на трек: «Исполнитель - Название».</li>
            </ol>
          </div>

          <input className="input" placeholder="Название плейлиста" value={plName} onChange={(e) => setPlName(e.target.value)} maxLength={100} />
          <label className="btn cursor-pointer">
            <FileText size={14} /> {fileName || 'Выбрать файл (.csv, .txt)'}
            <input type="file" accept=".csv,.txt,text/csv,text/plain" className="hidden" onChange={(e) => void onFile(e.target.files?.[0])} />
          </label>
          <textarea
            className="input min-h-40 font-mono text-xs"
            placeholder={'или вставьте список:\nLogic - 44 Bars\nJay-Z - 99 Problems'}
            value={text}
            onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button className="btn btn-primary" onClick={importFromText} disabled={!text.trim() || !!busy}>
            {busy ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" /> : null}
            Импортировать
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


--- ФАЙЛ: ./app/(app)/upload/page.tsx ---

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { api } from '@/lib/api';
import { UploadCloud, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

interface UploadItem {
  file: File;
  name: string;
  status: 'pending' | 'uploading' | 'success' | 'error';
  error?: string;
}

export default function UploadPage() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<UploadItem[]>([]);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);

  const handleFiles = (files: FileList | File[]) => {
    const newItems: UploadItem[] = Array.from(files).map((file) => ({
      file,
      name: file.name,
      status: 'pending',
    }));
    setItems((prev) => [...prev, ...newItems]);
  };

  const startUpload = async () => {
    const pendingItems = items.filter((i) => i.status === 'pending' || i.status === 'error');
    if (pendingItems.length === 0) return;

    setIsUploading(true);

    for (const item of pendingItems) {
      setItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((i) => (i.name === item.name ? { ...i, status: 'uploading' } : i))
      );

      try {
        const formData = new FormData();
        // Ключ обязательно должен быть 'files', так как multer ожидает upload.array('files')
        formData.append('files', item.file);

        await api<any>('/upload', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: formData,
        });

        setItems((prev) =>
          prev.map((i) => (i.name === item.name ? { ...i, status: 'success' } : i))
        );
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Upload error:', err);
        setItems((prev) =>
          prev.map((i) =>
            i.name === item.name
              ? { ...i, status: 'error', error: err?.message || 'Ошибка загрузки' }
              : i
          )
        );
      }
    }

    setIsUploading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full p-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto overflow-y-auto">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-pink-100 mb-6">Загрузка треков</h1>

      <div
        onDragOver={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          setIsDragging(true);
        }}
        onDragLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}
        onDrop={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          setIsDragging(false);
          if (e.dataTransfer.files) {
            handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files);
          }
        }}
        className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-2xl p-10 text-center transition-all ${
          isDragging
            ? 'border-pink-400 bg-pink-500/20'
            : 'border-pink-500/30 bg-pink-950/20 hover:border-pink-500/50'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
          <UploadCloud size={48} className="text-pink-400 animate-bounce" />
          <p className="text-pink-200 font-medium">Перетащите файлы сюда или нажмите для выбора</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-pink-300/50">Формат имени: Исполнитель - Название.mp3</p>
          <label className="mt-2 cursor-pointer bg-pink-500 hover:bg-pink-600 text-pink-950 font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-lg shadow-pink-500/20">
            Выбрать файлы
            <input
              type="file"
              multiple
              accept="audio/*"
              className="hidden"
              onChange={(e) => e.target.files && handleFiles(e.target.files)}
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      {items.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-pink-200/70">Выбрано файлов: {items.length}</span>
            <button
              onClick={startUpload}
              disabled={isUploading}
              className="bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-500 to-rose-500 hover:from-pink-600 hover:to-rose-600 text-white font-medium px-5 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm transition-all disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer shadow-md shadow-pink-950"
            >
              {isUploading ? 'Загрузка...' : 'Загрузить все'}
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-2">
            {items.map((item, idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-pink-950/30 border border-pink-500/15"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 truncate">
                  {item.status === 'success' && <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald-400 shrink-0" size={20} />}
                  {item.status === 'error' && <AlertCircle className="text-rose-400 shrink-0" size={20} />}
                  {item.status === 'uploading' && <Loader2 className="text-pink-400 shrink-0 animate-spin" size={20} />}
                  {item.status === 'pending' && <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 border-pink-500/30 shrink-0" />}
                  <span className="text-sm text-pink-100 truncate">{item.name}</span>
                </div>
                <span className="text-xs font-medium px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg">
                  {item.status === 'success' && <span className="text-emerald-300 bg-emerald-950/50 border border-emerald-500/20 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">Успешно</span>}
                  {item.status === 'error' && <span className="text-rose-300 bg-rose-950/50 border border-rose-500/20 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">{item.error || 'Ошибка'}</span>}
                  {item.status === 'uploading' && <span className="text-pink-300 bg-pink-950/50 border border-pink-500/20 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">Загрузка...</span>}
                  {item.status === 'pending' && <span className="text-pink-300/50">Ожидание</span>}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
